<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>记者报道，有人在 Polymarket 对其伊朗导弹报道下注后威胁要杀害他人。此事凸显在实时博彩市场中报道敏感国际议题时，记者面临的现实风险，以及安全与编辑独立性可能受到的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://translate.kagi.com/?from=en&amp;to=LinkedIn+speak' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kagi Translate now supports LinkedIn Speak as an output language</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 21:39 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47408703'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kagi Translate adds LinkedIn Speak as a translation target, enabling outputs in a professional LinkedIn-friendly register. This broadens its channel-aware capabilities, helping users tailor content for business networks and improve engagement on professional posts. It reflects growing demand for channel-specific personas in translation tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kagi Translate 增加了 LinkedIn Speak 作为输出语言，能把翻译结果呈现为专业、适合 LinkedIn 的语气。此举让内容更易在商业网络中引发共鸣，提升职业帖子的互动率。也反映了翻译工具越来越强调按渠道定制的能力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c70n2x7p22do' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Palestinian boy, 12, describes how Israeli forces killed his family in car</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>12岁巴勒斯坦男孩描述以色列军队在车内杀害家人</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 11:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47402950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47402950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 393</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

The testimony highlights civilian harm and the human cost of military operations, underscoring the volatility of frontline reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名12岁巴勒斯坦男孩讲述在一起车内事件中，他的家人被以色列军队杀害的经过。此证词突显冲突中的平民伤亡与人道代价，也反映出前线报道的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47460525' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47460525' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opencode.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenCode – Open source AI coding agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenCode：开源AI编码代理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 14:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47460525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47460525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 599</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47460525'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenCode is an open-source AI coding agent that helps developers automate routine coding tasks and explore AI-assisted workflows. By being open-source, it invites experimentation, customization, and transparency, contrasting with proprietary copilots. This matters for developers weighing privacy, control, and ecosystem when adopting AI coding tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenCode 是一个开源的 AI 编码代理，旨在帮助开发者自动化常规编码任务并尝试 AI 驱动的工作流。作为开源项目，它鼓励社区参与、定制和透明度，与闭源的代码助手形成对比。对于考虑隐私、控制权和生态系统的开发者来说，这是选择 AI 编码工具时一个重要考量。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47436950' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nypost.com/2026/03/18/us-news/afroman-found-not-liable-in-bizarre-ohio-defamation-case/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Afroman found not liable in defamation case</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Afroman 在诽谤案中被裁定对警察无责</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 02:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47436950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47436950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 690</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47436950'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Afroman was found not liable in a defamation suit brought by Ohio police who had raided his home. The ruling emphasizes the high standard for proving defamation when public figures discuss police conduct and may influence future entertainment-law disputes. This outcome matters for creators who voice controversial statements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Afroman 在俄亥俄州警察就突袭其家中提起的诽谤诉讼中被判无责任。判决强调涉及公共人物与执法行为的言论案件需满足较高的证明标准，可能影响未来相关诉讼的走向，并对娱乐行业有一定参考意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nypost.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/03/Comp_-50.jpg?quality=75&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://nypost.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/03/Comp_-50.jpg?quality=75&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47442690' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47442690' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/03/google-details-new-24-hour-process-to-sideload-unverified-android-apps/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google details new 24-hour process to sideload unverified Android apps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌公布 24 小时内可 sideload 未验证 Android 应用的新流程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 10:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47442690' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47442690' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1237</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47442690'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google detailed a 24-hour window that allows sideloading of unverified Android apps to accelerate testing and internal distribution outside the Play ecosystem. While it speeds iteration, it broadens the attack surface and prompts heightened security and policy safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌公布了一项 24 小时窗口，允许 sideload 未经验证的 Android 应用以加速测试和内部分发。此举虽提升迭代速度，但也扩大了潜在攻击面，需加强安全与策略层面的防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/developer-identity-hero-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/developer-identity-hero-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47392084' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47392084' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.michaelgeist.ca/2026/03/a-tale-of-two-bills-lawful-access-returns-with-changes-to-warrantless-access-but-dangerous-backdoor-surveillance-risks-remains/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bill C-22, the Lawful Access Act: Dangerous backdoor surveillance risks remain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bill C-22：合法获取法案——后门监控风险仍存</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 14:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47392084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 988</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47392084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 328</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47392084'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bill C-22 would expand law enforcement access to data, with changes that still allow warrantless backdoor surveillance risks. Privacy advocates warn that even with reforms, the bill could enable broad, covert monitoring across digital communications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bill C-22 将扩大执法对数据的获取权限，尽管作出了一些修改，后门监控风险仍然存在。隐私倡导者警告这类立法可能让大范围、非授权的监控成为现实，侵害公民数字隐私。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.michaelgeist.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/screenshot_4102.png' data-full='https://www.michaelgeist.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/screenshot_4102.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47454341' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47454341' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/03/im-ok-being-left-behind-thanks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m OK being left behind, thanks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谢谢，我愿意落后一点</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 06:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 968</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 751</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47454341'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many readers may interpret as a stance against the AI arms race. It argues for a slower, more deliberate pace focused on reliability and user welfare. In practice, it challenges the assumption that faster is always better in tech development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者不愿被技术浪潮淹没，选择慢一些、稳妥前进。强调稳定性和隐私比追逐热点更重要。这样的观点提醒在快速迭代的行业里，理性节奏同样值得关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/Smile-Metaverse.webp' data-full='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/Smile-Metaverse.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47423647' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47423647' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cs.unc.edu/~stotts/COMP590-059-f24/robsrules.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rob Pike’s Rules of Programming (1989)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rob Pike 的五条编程法则</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 02:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47423647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 947</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47423647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 441</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47423647'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Practical guidance from Rob Pike emphasizes keeping code simple, correct, and maintainable. The five rules center on readable interfaces, orthogonality, and constructing reliable, easily reasoned systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rob Pike 的五条编程法则集中呈现了实用的编程智慧，强调构建可靠软件的重要性。核心内容包括简洁、可读接口、正交性，以及易于推理和维护的代码。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.otherstrangeness.com/2026/03/14/have-a-fucking-website/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Have a fucking website</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一定要有一个网站</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 20:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47421442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 889</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47421442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 505</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47421442'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Having a dedicated website gives you a stable home for your projects, portfolio, and experiments, independent of platform policies. A minimal static site with a custom domain can dramatically improve your online credibility and discoverability for developers and creators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拥有专属网站为项目、简历与实验成果提供稳定归宿，不依赖社交媒体平台。即使是简单的静态站点搭配自定义域名，也能显著提升可发现性与专业形象。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.otherstrangeness.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/someone-please-tell-me-where-this-image-comes-from-v0-on5002nlkxyd1.jpeg-e1773478577120.webp' data-full='https://www.otherstrangeness.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/someone-please-tell-me-where-this-image-comes-from-v0-on5002nlkxyd1.jpeg-e1773478577120.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47410870' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47410870' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gadgetreview.com/reddit-user-uncovers-who-is-behind-metas-2b-lobbying-for-invasive-age-verification-tech' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reddit User Uncovers Who Is Behind Meta&#x27;s $2B Lobbying for Age Verification Tech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Reddit 用户揭露谁在为 Meta 的年龄验证技术投入 20 亿美元进行游说</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 03:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47410870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 871</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47410870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47410870'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Reddit user exposes who is behind Meta’s $2 billion lobbying push for age verification tech. The reveal sheds light on the opaque networks that drive big-tech policy influence and raises questions about privacy, consent, and the effectiveness of age checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位 Reddit 用户披露了推动 Meta 就年龄验证技术进行20亿美元游说的幕后主体。这一揭露揭示了驱动大型科技公司政策影响的隐蔽网络，并引发关于隐私、同意和年龄核验有效性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/38-artapixel-meta-logo-icon-3d-social-media-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/38-artapixel-meta-logo-icon-3d-social-media-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47467537' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47467537' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/3/20/some-things-just-take-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Some things just take time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有些事就是需要时间</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 07:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 825</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 268</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47467537'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Patience is essential for long-running tech work; complex problems don&#x27;t yield quick wins. The piece argues that taking time to iterate and learn is often the difference between failure and breakthrough.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在长期的技术工作中，耐心至关重要；复杂问题难以快速解决。文章指出，慢慢迭代和积累经验往往比急于求成带来更大成功的可能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-03-20-some-things-just-take-time-social.png' data-full='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-03-20-some-things-just-take-time-social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47470991' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47470991' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.dyne.org/child-protection-is-not-access-control/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do Not Turn Child Protection into Internet Access Control</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要把儿童保护变成互联网访问控制</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 13:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47470991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 821</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47470991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 433</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47470991'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Guardrails for child protection should be precise and targeted, not a pretext for blanket internet access control. Overly broad censorship risks chilling freedom online while failing to stop abuse. The piece calls for privacy-preserving, surgical safeguards instead.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>儿童保护政策不应成为全面的网络访问控制的借口。宽泛的封锁容易压制在线自由，并未有效阻止滥用。应采用更精准、透明的隐私保护措施与安全防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.dyne.org/content/images/size/w1200/2026/03/90d2be8e-f920-4ac4-9c8a-092bc2ce88bf.png' data-full='https://news.dyne.org/content/images/size/w1200/2026/03/90d2be8e-f920-4ac4-9c8a-092bc2ce88bf.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47433058' data-tags='Politics,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47433058' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pew.org/en/research-and-analysis/articles/2026/03/18/austins-surge-of-new-housing-construction-drove-down-rents' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Austin’s surge of new housing construction drove down rents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>奥斯汀新建住房激增致租金下降</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 17:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47433058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 793</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47433058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 973</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47433058'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Austin’s housing boom kept rents in check as a wave of new units came online, softening affordability even as demand from tech workers remains high. The finding highlights how supplying more units can influence urban affordability and inform policy debates on zoning and infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>奥斯汀的住房供给激增带动新建单位上线，抵消了需求，导致租金走低。此现象反映出供给侧扩张对城市可负担性的直接影响，可能影响土地使用、基础设施和住房政策的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pew.org/-/media/post-launch-images/2026/03/gettyimages1446174163jpgmaster/16x9_m.jpg' data-full='https://www.pew.org/-/media/post-launch-images/2026/03/gettyimages1446174163jpgmaster/16x9_m.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47390945' data-tags='Web,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47390945' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thatshubham.com/blog/news-audit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 49MB web page</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>49MB 的网页</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 12:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47390945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 792</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47390945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 353</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47390945'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The page weighs in at 49MB, serving as a cautionary example of how modern sites can balloon with images, scripts, and third-party resources. The write-up walks through auditing approaches to identify bloated bundles, optimize critical paths, and pare back assets without wrecking UX. Takeaway: page size is a first-class performance lever, and thoughtful auditing matters for both users and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该网页达到 49MB，揭示现代站点如何被图片、脚本和第三方资源膨胀。文中提供审计方法，帮助识别冗余资源、优化关键渲染路径，并在不破坏用户体验的前提下精简资产。要点：页面大小是性能的重要变量，细致的审计对用户和开发者都重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thatshubham.com/img/news-audit-og.jpg' data-full='https://thatshubham.com/img/news-audit-og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47413876' data-tags='Security,Gaming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47413876' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/console-gaming/microsofts-unhackable-xbox-one-has-been-hacked-by-bliss-the-2013-console-finally-fell-to-voltage-glitching-allowing-the-loading-of-unsigned-code-at-every-level' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft&#x27;s &#x27;unhackable&#x27; Xbox One has been hacked by &#x27;Bliss&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软所谓的“不可破解”的 Xbox One 被 Bliss 破解</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 08:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47413876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 789</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47413876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 291</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47413876'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrated a voltage-glitch attack against Microsoft&#x27;s Xbox One, breaking the console&#x27;s claimed &#x27;unhackable&#x27; security. The hack lets unsigned code run at various privilege levels, undermining hardware protections and enabling homebrew or piracy-style access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员通过电压干扰对 Xbox One 展开攻击，打破了其“不可破解”的安全宣称。该漏洞使未签名代码在不同权限级别下得以运行，削弱了硬件防护，并可能开启自制软件或盗版访问。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/rZ7dRyfYH7ZD7yApRmNiva-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/rZ7dRyfYH7ZD7yApRmNiva-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47450478' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47450478' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/arxiv-pioneering-preprint-server-declares-independence-cornell' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ArXiv declares independence from Cornell</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ArXiv宣布独立于康奈尔大学</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 21:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47450478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 784</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47450478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47450478'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ArXiv, the long-running open-access preprint server, is shedding its longtime host by shifting governance away from Cornell. The move signals a push toward community-led stewardship and diversified funding as arXiv seeks long-term sustainability. For researchers, it could mean changes in governance, policy, and how quickly new features roll out.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作为长期负责的开放获取预印本服务器，ArXiv 正在将治理从康奈尔大学转向独立管理。此次转变旨在实现更具社区驱动色彩的治理结构和多元化资金来源，以确保长期可持续性。对于研究者而言，未来在治理、政策和新功能上线速度等方面可能出现变化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47404796' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47404796' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/leanstral' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leanstral: Open-source agent for trustworthy coding and formal proof engineering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Leanstral：用于可信编码与形式化证明工程的开源代理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 13:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47404796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 763</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47404796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47404796'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Leanstral is an open-source agent designed to assist developers with trustworthy coding and formal proof engineering. It integrates AI-assisted coding with formal verification tooling to improve software correctness and reliability, making rigorous development workflows more accessible.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Leanstral 是一个面向开发者的开源代理，旨在协助进行可信编码与形式化证明工程。它将 AI 辅助编码与形式化验证工具结合，提升软件正确性与可信度，让严格的软件工程工作流更易落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/img/mistral-cover.png' data-full='https://mistral.ai/img/mistral-cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47444319' data-tags='Business,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47444319' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepdelver.substack.com/p/delve-fake-compliance-as-a-service' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Delve – Fake Compliance as a Service</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Delve：假合规即服务</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 12:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47444319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 756</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47444319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47444319'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delve dives into the emerging &#x27;Fake Compliance as a Service&#x27; phenomenon, critiquing services that generate pretend attestations or simplified controls to appease audits. The piece warns about integrity risks for users, regulators, and auditors when compliance is commodified.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Delve 深度剖析新兴的“假合规即服务”现象，批评那些提供虚假合规证明或简化控制的服务。文章警示当合规被商品化时，对用户、监管机构和审计方构成的信任与合规性风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47410542' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47410542' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kagi.com/smallweb/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kagi Small Web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kagi 推出 Small Web 小型网络</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 02:53 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/finance/us-sec-preparing-eliminate-quarterly-reporting-requirement-wsj-says-2026-03-16/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US SEC preparing to scrap quarterly reporting requirement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国SEC拟取消季度报告要求</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 17:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47406779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 743</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47406779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 447</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47406779'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission is preparing to eliminate the mandatory quarterly reporting requirement for public companies. This could cut compliance costs and reduce quarterly volatility, but may shift investor expectations toward annual disclosures and alter how markets price near-term risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国证监会正筹划取消强制性季度财报，可能减轻上市公司合规负担，降低季度业绩波动。此举或改变投资者对时点披露的期待，并影响市场对短期风险的定价和分析方法。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47397593' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47397593' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/political-science/articles/10.3389/fpos.2026.1779810/full' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Corruption erodes social trust more in democracies than in autocracies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>腐败在民主国家对社会信任的侵蚀比专制国家更严重</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 04:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 717</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 380</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47397593'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new study finds corruption erodes social trust more in democratic countries than in autocracies. The result suggests that transparency and accountability mechanisms in democracies can backfire when corruption spreads, hurting trust in institutions more than in tightly controlled regimes. The finding matters for governance, civic tech, and how platforms rely on public trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究显示，腐败对社会信任的侵蚀在民主国家比在专制国家更明显。这一发现提示，民主制度的透明与问责机制在某些情境下可能放大信任的下滑，对治理、公民科技以及依赖信任的平台都具重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2csxpduxe849s.cloudfront.net/media/E32629C6-9347-4F84-81FEAEF7BFA342B3/CF13FC93-1D3A-473F-8D8F9F692E417F16/5974D84F-C240-47A0-B44DDB68A3E39BD6/WebsiteWebP_XL-FPOS_Main Visual_Purple_Website.webp' data-full='https://d2csxpduxe849s.cloudfront.net/media/E32629C6-9347-4F84-81FEAEF7BFA342B3/CF13FC93-1D3A-473F-8D8F9F692E417F16/5974D84F-C240-47A0-B44DDB68A3E39BD6/WebsiteWebP_XL-FPOS_Main Visual_Purple_Website.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PC Gamer 在一篇 37MB 的文章中推荐 RSS 阅读器，但文稿似乎不断在下载</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 11:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 679</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 320</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47480507'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PC Gamer curates a selection of RSS readers in a long, 37MB feature. The piece itself is unexpectedly download-heavy, turning a simple recommendation into a test of browser patience. The underlying takeaway is that enthusiasts still crave lightweight, dependable feed readers in an era of endless content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PC Gamer 选出一批 RSS 阅读器的清单，但这篇 37MB 的文章本身却像在持续下载内容，考验浏览器耐心。文章透露在信息过载的时代，用户仍然需要轻量、可靠的订阅工具。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openrocket.info/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenRocket</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenRocket：开源火箭设计与仿真工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 05:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47386703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 656</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47386703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47386703'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenRocket is an open-source rocket design and flight simulation package that lets hobbyists, students, and researchers model aerodynamics, stability, and trajectories before launch. It helps verify designs and teaches rocketry principles without expensive hardware. The project embodies the practical value of open scientific tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenRocket 是一个开源的火箭设计与飞行仿真工具，用户能在发射前建模气动、稳定性和轨迹。它便于爱好者、学生和研究者在没有昂贵设备的情况下验证设计、学习火箭学原理。该项目体现了开源科学工具的实用价值。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lemonde.fr/en/international/article/2026/03/20/stravaleaks-france-s-aircraft-carrier-located-in-real-time-by-le-monde-through-fitness-app_6751640_4.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France&#x27;s aircraft carrier located in real time by Le Monde through fitness app</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>勒蒙德通过健身应用实现法国航母的实时定位</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 06:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 629</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 514</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47453942'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Le Monde investigation shows real-time tracking of France&#x27;s aircraft carrier via fitness app data, revealing how public location sharing can leak sensitive security information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>勒蒙德的调查显示，公开的健身应用数据可能让法国航母的位置在实时暴露，暴露了公共数据对国家安全的潜在威胁。此事凸显了个人位置信息在缺乏隐私保护的情况下对安全的影响，促使对数据共享进行审视。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们对 Windows 品质的承诺</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47459296'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft outlines its plan to improve Windows quality through deeper testing, more stable updates, and better compatibility, aiming to restore user trust after a string of issues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软阐述通过更深入的测试、更加稳定的更新和更好的兼容性来提升 Windows 质量，旨在重获用户信任。文章强调软件质量在竞争激烈的操作系统市场中的关键作用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47434047' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47434047' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://haskellforall.com/2026/03/a-sufficiently-detailed-spec-is-code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A sufficiently detailed spec is code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>足够详细的规格就是代码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 19:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47434047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47434047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 327</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47434047'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Detailed specifications can serve as concrete, testable artifacts that drive implementation without separate code. When specs are precise, teams can simulate behavior and verify outcomes directly from the docs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>足够详尽的规格本身就能成为可执行的产物，推动实现、测试与仿真，模糊了文档与代码的界线。把规范当作代码来管理，可以用自动化校验与模拟来验证行为。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://haskellforall.com/2026/03/a-sufficiently-detailed-spec-is-code/opengraph-image?133960dfd41055ac' data-full='https://haskellforall.com/2026/03/a-sufficiently-detailed-spec-is-code/opengraph-image?133960dfd41055ac' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47390817' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47390817' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.chrome.com/blog/chrome-devtools-mcp-debug-your-browser-session' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chrome DevTools MCP (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chrome DevTools 的 MCP（2025）——调试浏览器会话的新方式</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 12:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47390817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 586</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47390817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47390817'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome DevTools introduces MCP-based tooling to debug browser sessions more comprehensively, enabling cross-process inspection and more seamless workflows for developers. The update aims to improve transparency and troubleshooting in complex web apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome DevTools 推出 2025 版的 MCP 调试工作流，使开发者能够更全面地调试浏览器会话、跨进程诊断问题。新工 具提升了对复杂网页应用的可观测性与排错效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developer.chrome.com/static/blog/chrome-devtools-mcp-debug-your-browser-session/image/hero.png' data-full='https://developer.chrome.com/static/blog/chrome-devtools-mcp-debug-your-browser-session/image/hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47470773' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47470773' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tinygrad.org/#tinybox' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tinybox – A powerful computer for deep learning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tinybox：用于深度学习的强大小型电脑</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 13:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47470773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 583</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47470773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 339</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47470773'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tinybox packs substantial compute into a compact box, enabling on-device deep learning experiments without relying on the cloud. It targets researchers, hobbyists, and edge deployments seeking serious compute with a small footprint.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tinybox 将强大算力压缩进小巧盒子，支持本地深度学习试验，无需依赖云端。面向希望在紧凑空间内获得高性能计算的研究者、爱好者与边缘部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tinygrad.org/assets/tinybox.jpg' data-full='https://tinygrad.org/assets/tinybox.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47388676' data-tags='AI,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47388676' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sebastianraschka.com/llm-architecture-gallery/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLM Architecture Gallery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLM 架构画廊</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 09:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 566</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47388676'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LLM Architecture Gallery presents a visual catalog of common and emerging LLM architectures, from vanilla transformer stacks to retrieval-augmented and modular designs. It helps engineers compare trade-offs in latency, memory, and accuracy. Takeaway: architecture choices shape capabilities and cost, and a visual guide helps map the design space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LLM 架构画廊以可视化形式整理了常见与新兴的模型架构，从基础的 Transformer 堆栈到检索增强和模块化设计，帮助工程师对比在延迟、内存与精度等维度的权衡。要点：架构选择决定能力和成本，直观的对比有助于把握设计空间。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47478401' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47478401' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bramcohen.com/p/manyana' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The future of version control</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>版本控制的未来</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 08:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47478401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 562</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47478401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 311</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47478401'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Visionaries describe the next phase of version control beyond Git-style systems, spotlighting peer-to-peer collaboration, cryptographic integrity, and scalable, offline-friendly workflows. The takeaway is that new paradigms could change how teams coordinate, verify history, and scale development. For technologists, this signals a shift in toolchains and trust conventions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论了超越现有版本控制系统的新范式，强调点对点协作、加密完整性和可离线扩展的工作流。核心在于新机制可能改变团队的协作、历史验证和开发扩展的方式。对开发者来说，这预示着工具链与信任体系的变革。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!93xj!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbramcohen.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1733548744%26version%3D9' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!93xj!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbramcohen.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1733548744%26version%3D9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47422942' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nightingale.cafe/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nightingale – open-source karaoke app that works with any song on your computer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nightingale——开源的本地音乐卡拉OK应用，能用你电脑上的任意歌曲</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 01:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47422942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 555</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47422942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47422942'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nightingale is an open-source karaoke app that works with local music files on your computer. It offers an offline-first, privacy-respecting alternative to cloud-based karaoke tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nightingale 是一款开源卡拉OK应用，可直接使用电脑本地的音乐文件进行演唱，无需联网服务。它体现了离线优先的音乐工具和对隐私的重视。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nightingale.cafe/og-image.png' data-full='https://nightingale.cafe/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47437655' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47437655' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daringfireball.net/2026/03/your_frustration_is_the_product' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Your frustration is the product”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>『你的挫折就是产品』</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 04:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47437655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 545</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47437655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 317</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47437655'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that many products monetize user frustration through friction and attention traps. It urges designers to rethink engagement strategies and prioritize user autonomy and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为许多产品通过制造挫折感、设计摩擦和注意力陷阱来变现。呼吁设计师重新审视参与策略，优先考虑用户自主性与透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://daringfireball.net/graphics/df-wide-card.png' data-full='https://daringfireball.net/graphics/df-wide-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47408205' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47408205' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apenwarr.ca/log/20260316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every layer of review makes you 10x slower</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每多一层评审，速度就会慢十倍</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 20:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47408205' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 540</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47408205' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47408205'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An argument that added review layers multiply latency, urging leaner processes. The post argues for reducing handoffs and embracing light-weight reviews, automation, and clear ownership to keep velocity high.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为每增加一层评审都会让交付速度慢十倍，评审层级的叠加带来显著延迟。文章主张减少不必要的环节，加强自动化和明确的职责分工，以维持高开发节奏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://apenwarr.ca/img/ave-home.jpg' data-full='https://apenwarr.ca/img/ave-home.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47401879' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47401879' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kevinboone.me/small_web_is_big.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The “small web” is bigger than you might think</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“小型网络”比你想象的更大</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 10:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47401879'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful look at the indie or small web beyond big platforms, highlighting personal sites, RSS, and distributed hosting. It argues the ecosystem is bigger and more varied than it appears, with privacy-friendly projects continuing to grow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对独立/小型网络的深入观察，聚焦个人站点、RSS 和分布式托管等现象。指出这个生态系统其实比外界想象的要大、更加多元，隐私友好型项目仍在成长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kevinboone.me/img/web.png' data-full='https://kevinboone.me/img/web.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47441546' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47441546' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/KittenML/KittenTTS' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Three new Kitten TTS models – smallest less than 25MB</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：三个新的 Kitten TTS 模型——最小版小于 25MB</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 08:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47441546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47441546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47441546'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three new Kitten TTS models are introduced, notable for ultra-small footprints; the smallest model is under 25MB. This showcases the potential for on-device, offline text-to-speech on resource-constrained hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示的三个 Kitten TTS 模型体积极小，最小版本不到 25MB，凸显在边缘设备上实现高质量文本转语音的可行性。小体积模型有助于离线应用、低带宽环境，以及快速本地化部署。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47464818' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47464818' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/03/blocking-internet-archive-wont-stop-ai-it-will-erase-webs-historical-record' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blocking Internet Archive Won&#x27;t Stop AI, but Will Erase Web&#x27;s Historical Record</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会阻止 AI，但会抹去网络的历史记录</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 00:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47464818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 533</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47464818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47464818'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blocking Internet Archive won&#x27;t curb AI development, and models will still train on data from other sources. It would erase a vital historical snapshot of the web, harming researchers, journalists, and accountability. Smarter data-use policies are preferable to outright blackouts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会遏制 AI 发展，模型仍会从其他来源获取数据。这会抹去网络的宝贵历史快照，损害研究、报道和问责能力。应采用更聪明的数据使用政策，而不是全面封锁。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/internet-archive-servers-by-jason-scott.jpg' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/internet-archive-servers-by-jason-scott.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47401809' data-tags='Health,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47401809' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rexrodeo/american-healthcare-conundrum' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The American Healthcare Conundrum</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国医疗保健难题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 10:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 512</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 613</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47401809'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The American Healthcare Conundrum offers a data-driven look at why U.S. healthcare remains so expensive and fragmented despite high spending. The project highlights cost drivers, insurance complexity, and misaligned incentives, providing a practical lens for policy and tech-centered fixes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一份基于数据的分析，揭示美国医疗体系为何价格高昂且仍然碎片化。项目聚焦成本驱动因素、保险体系的复杂性和激励错位，提供面向政策、技术与医疗服务改进的实践视角。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1170412263/5f53d927-62aa-402c-8b6c-7356e0f19945' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1170412263/5f53d927-62aa-402c-8b6c-7356e0f19945' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47402640' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://engineering.fb.com/2026/03/02/data-infrastructure/investing-in-infrastructure-metas-renewed-commitment-to-jemalloc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta’s renewed commitment to jemalloc</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 再次承诺优化 jemalloc</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 11:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47402640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 510</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47402640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47402640'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta renews its commitment to jemalloc, the memory allocator powering its data infrastructure. The move signals continued focus on performance, latency, and memory efficiency at scale, with potential upstream contributions benefiting other projects that rely on jemalloc.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 再次承诺继续优化 jemalloc，这一为数据基础设施提供分配内存的组件在大规模系统中扮演关键角色。此举表明 Meta 将持续提升性能与内存效率，并可能向 jemalloc 的开源社区带来贡献，惠及依赖该分配器的其他项目。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://engineering.fb.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/jemalloc-at-Meta-Hero.png' data-full='https://engineering.fb.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/jemalloc-at-Meta-Hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/03/18/fbi-is-buying-location-data-to-track-us-citizens-kash-patel-wyden/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FBI is buying location data to track US citizens, director confirms</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 13:09 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47430797'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397574' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 499</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397574' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 252</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal ode to FreeBSD, praising its performance, security model, and the stability of its ports and tooling. The piece argues FreeBSD remains a compelling choice for servers and hobbyists who value control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享对 FreeBSD 的热爱，称赞其性能、安全模型及稳健的包管理与工具链。文章认为对追求可控性和稳定性的服务器与爱好者来说，FreeBSD 仍然值得一试。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一个离线优先的知识项目，确保在断网或审查环境中也能访问信息，强调通过本地化数据策略提升知识的韧性与可携带性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47400060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 493</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47400060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 351</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Karpathy 的美国就业市场可视化工具绘制了科技领域的需求、薪资和地理分布，为判断 AI 与软件技能的市场前景提供数据化视角。对职业规划很有参考价值。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47426057' data-tags='Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47426057' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.propublica.org/article/microsoft-cloud-fedramp-cybersecurity-government' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Despite doubts, federal cyber experts approved Microsoft cloud service</a></p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fidget-spinner.github.io/posts/jit-on-track.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Python 3.15&#x27;s JIT is now back on track</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python 3.15 的 JIT 已重新回到正轨</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 11:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 476</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 297</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47416486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Python 3.15&#x27;s JIT development has resumed, signaling renewed momentum for speeding up Python without breaking existing C-extensions. The effort aims to bring a practical JIT to CPython, potentially narrowing performance gaps with PyPy and boosting workloads across data science and web apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Python 3.15 的 JIT 开发已重新启动，意味着在不破坏现有扩展的前提下提升 Python 运行速度的努力又有了新动能。该项目力求在 CPython 中引入实用的即时编译，或缩小与 PyPy 的性能差距，并提升数据科学与 Web 应用等场景的性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fidget-spinner.github.io/media/jit-on-track/brrr-20260317.png' data-full='https://fidget-spinner.github.io/media/jit-on-track/brrr-20260317.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS 将继续对所有人开放使用，无需个人信息</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 14:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47482217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 474</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47482217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS reiterates its privacy-first approach, stressing that you can use the OS without providing personal information. The note highlights the trade-offs in keeping the ecosystem usable without sign-ins or telemetry, and what this means for typical users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 继续坚持隐私优先理念，强调无需提供个人信息即可使用系统。文章点出在保持无需账户或数据传输的前提下，生态系统所需的权衡以及对普通用户的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anomalyco/opencode/pull/18186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic takes legal action against OpenCode</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic has filed a lawsuit against OpenCode, signaling a new legal tussle in the AI ecosystem. The action highlights ongoing battles over training data, IP, and competitive practices that could influence how AI startups operate and disclose information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 对 OpenCode 提起诉讼，标志着 AI 领域新的法律博弈。此举凸显关于训练数据、知识产权和竞争行为的持续之争，可能影响 AI 初创公司的运营方式与信息披露要求。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47476315' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stevekrouse.com/precision' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reports of code&#x27;s death are greatly exaggerated</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于代码死亡的传闻被大大夸大</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 04:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 446</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 335</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47476315'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Code isn&#x27;t dead; AI tools will augment but not replace human software craftsmen. The piece argues that precise, maintainable code, rigorous testing, and clear design remain essential as automation shifts routine work. In practice, expect a continued emphasis on tooling that preserves correctness and developer control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管有 AI 辅助写码的趋势，代码并未消亡。高质量、可维护的代码、严谨的测试和明确的设计仍然是关键，因为自动化会改变工作方式但需要人类把控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imagedelivery.net/iHX6Ovru0O7AjmyT5yZRoA/f7e9e621-0fad-4c3a-a65b-d16832c02b00/public' data-full='https://imagedelivery.net/iHX6Ovru0O7AjmyT5yZRoA/f7e9e621-0fad-4c3a-a65b-d16832c02b00/public' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47406160' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47406160' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://forums.tigsource.com/index.php?topic=40832.msg1363742#msg1363742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Return of the Obra Dinn: spherical mapped dithering for a 1bpp first-person game</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《Obra Dinn》的回归：用于 1bpp 第一人称游戏的球面映射抖动</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 15:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47406160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 431</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47406160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47406160'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This post discusses applying spherical mapped dithering to achieve 1-bit-per-pixel visuals in a first-person game, connecting to Return of the Obra Dinn. The technique demonstrates how low-bit rendering can yield striking aesthetics for constrained engines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出在 1bpp 的第一人称游戏中使用球面映射抖动来实现风格独特的绘制效果，借由低比特深度渲染实现视觉冲击。此思路展示了资源受限的场景也能获得鲜明美术风格，或为复古/低配置引擎提供参考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47480886' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 12:02 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47400089' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47400089' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.better-simple.com/django/2026/03/16/give-django-your-time-and-money/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Give Django your time and money, not your tokens</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>给 Django 投入时间和金钱，而不是代币</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 08:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47400089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 424</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47400089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47400089'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Contribution to Django should come from real-world support—patches, code reviews, and sponsorship—rather than token-based schemes. Tokenized funding introduces volatility and governance risk that undermines a long-lived open source project. Real sponsorship and hands-on effort remain the reliable backbone.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Django 的长期发展，作者主张通过实际贡献来支撑，包括修补、代码评审和赞助，而不是代币化的资助方式。代币化融资容易带来波动和治理风险，对这个重要的开源项目并不友好。真正可靠的支撑来自实际的赞助与人力投入。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.better-simple.com/assets/images/2026/03/roland_scratching_post.jpg' data-full='https://www.better-simple.com/assets/images/2026/03/roland_scratching_post.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47388658' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47388658' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gitlab.com/IsolatedOctopi/nvidia_greenboost' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia greenboost: transparently extend GPU VRAM using system RAM/NVMe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NVIDIA GreenBoost：透明扩展显存，利用系统内存/ NVMe</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 08:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388658' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 415</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388658' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47388658'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GreenBoost proposes using host RAM and NVMe as a transparent extension of GPU VRAM, enabling larger-than-GPU memory workloads with potential performance costs. The project highlights memory management tradeoffs that could influence future GPU design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GreenBoost 提议将主机内存与 NVMe 作为显存的透明扩展，使在显存受限时也能处理更大规模的工作负载，代价是潜在的性能开销和延迟。该思路若落地，可能影响未来 GPU 的内存管理策略和驱动设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gitlab.com/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' data-full='https://gitlab.com/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bsky.app/profile/chriso-wiki.bsky.social/post/3mhfsau25uk2f' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Denmark was reportedly preparing for full-scale war with the US over Greenland</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 04:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47437782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47437782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 585</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rumors suggest Denmark contemplated full-scale war with the US over Greenland, reflecting shifting strategic dynamics in the North Atlantic. The article uses that claim to discuss how leaks, geopolitics, and resource interests can inflame headlines. It cautions readers to weigh source credibility in sensational claims.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称丹麦正为与美国就格陵兰问题展开全面战争做准备，暴露北大西洋区域的紧张局势。文章借此讨论地缘政治、资源争夺与联盟关系的敏感性，同时提醒对这一传闻的来源要保持怀疑。</p>
