<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47385935' data-tags='Hardware,Science,Space,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47385935' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/novatic14/MANPADS-System-Launcher-and-Rocket' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>$96 3D-printed rocket that recalculates its mid-air trajectory using a $5 sensor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>96 美元的 3D 打印火箭利用 5 美元传感器在空中重新计算轨迹</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 03:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47385935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 395</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47385935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 350</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47385935'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A $96 3D-printed rocket uses a $5 sensor to recalculate its trajectory mid-flight, showcasing how affordable components enable advanced control experiments. It highlights both impressive tinkering and the safety/regulatory gaps between hobbyist builds and regulated rocketry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个价值 96 美元的 3D 打印火箭，利用 5 美元传感器在飞行中重新计算轨迹，展示了低成本部件如何支持高端控制实验。此举凸显了爱好者探索的惊人性，同时也暴露了从业余试验到受监管火箭活动之间的安全与法规差距。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f62c46209929390f927d05538aa94c4229c25d30f05f968c0c28719bfb13d6a9/novatic14/MANPADS-System-Launcher-and-Rocket' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f62c46209929390f927d05538aa94c4229c25d30f05f968c0c28719bfb13d6a9/novatic14/MANPADS-System-Launcher-and-Rocket' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47448524' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47448524' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.claude.com/docs/en/channels' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Push events into a running session with channels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过通道将事件推送到正在运行的会话</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 17:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47448524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47448524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47448524'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Push events into an active session using channels, enabling asynchronous event streams to feed a running process. This pattern is useful for building interactive tools, REPLs, or simulations that need live data without blocking the main thread.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过通道将事件推送到正在运行的会话，使异步事件流可以实时进入一个正在执行的进程。该模式便于构建需要实时数据的交互式工具、REPL 或仿真，避免阻塞主线程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DAutomation%26appearance%3Dsystem%26title%3DPush%2Bevents%2Binto%2Ba%2Brunning%2Bsession%2Bwith%2Bchannels%26description%3DUse%2Bchannels%2Bto%2Bpush%2Bmessages%252C%2Balerts%252C%2Band%2Bwebhooks%2Binto%2Byour%2BClaude%2BCode%2Bsession%2Bfrom%2Ban%2BMCP%2Bserver.%2BForward%2BCI%2Bresults%252C%2Bchat%2Bmessages%252C%2Band%2Bmonitoring%2Bevents%2Bs%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fc5r9_6tjPMzFdDDT%252Flogo%252Flight.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Dc5r9_6tjPMzFdDDT%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D78fd01ff4f4340295a4f66e2ea54903c%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fc5r9_6tjPMzFdDDT%252Flogo%252Fdark.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Dc5r9_6tjPMzFdDDT%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D1298a0c3b3a1da603b190d0de0e31712%26primaryColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26lightColor%3D%2523D4A27F%26darkColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26backgroundLight%3D%2523FDFDF7%26backgroundDark%3D%252309090B&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' <div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47474255' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47474255' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://radar.cloudflare.com/domains/domain/archive.today' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare flags archive.today as &quot;C&amp;C/Botnet&quot;; no longer resolves via 1.1.1.2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare 将 archive.today 标记为 C&amp;C/Botnet；不再通过 1.1.1.2 解析</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 20:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47474255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 393</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47474255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 291</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47474255'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare flags archive.today as C&amp;C/Botnet and disables DNS resolution via 1.1.1.2, disrupting access for users relying on that resolver.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 将 archive.today 标记为 C&amp;C/Botnet，并停止通过 1.1.1.2 进行解析，导致使用该解析器的用户无法访问。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47416543' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47416543' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://practical.engineering/blog/2026/3/17/the-los-angeles-aqueduct-is-wild' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Los Angeles Aqueduct Is Wild</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>洛杉矶引水渠真是惊人</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 11:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 387</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47416543'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Los Angeles Aqueduct is portrayed as an engineering marvel with complex history and massive environmental consequences, from long gravity‑fed pipelines to major landscape alteration. The piece balances admiration for the engineering with scrutiny of the social and ecological costs of diverting water at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中将洛杉矶引水渠描述为一项既惊人又复杂的工程奇迹，涉及长距离重力输水和对地貌的大规模改造。文章在赞叹工程成就的同时，也审视了大规模调水对社会与生态造成的代价。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/55ac34e4e4b0170ef54be632/55ac3907e4b03d1c8c9deff1/69b9a04591992c11b5f51309/1773772948931/LA+Aqueduct+%28color%29.jpg?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/55ac34e4e4b0170ef54be632/55ac3907e4b03d1c8c9deff1/69b9a04591992c11b5f51309/1773772948931/LA+Aqueduct+%28color%29.jpg?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47438001' data-tags='Legal,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47438001' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/03/19/us/afroman-trial-lemon-cake-verdict.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Afroman Wins Civil Trial over Use of Police Raid Footage in His Music Videos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Afroman 就在其音乐视频中使用警方突袭录像的民事诉讼获胜</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 05:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47438001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 385</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47438001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47438001'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Afroman won a civil trial over the use of police raid footage in his music videos. The ruling clarifies limits on using publicly captured or sensitive footage in art and could affect how artists source and license such material.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>说唱歌手 Afroman 在一宗涉及在其音乐视频中使用警方突袭录像的民事诉讼中获胜。法院裁决厘清了在艺术创作中使用公开或敏感录像的边界，或影响艺人对该类素材的获取与授权。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47464134' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47464134' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pbxscience.com/ubuntu-26-04-ends-46-years-of-silent-sudo-passwords/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ubuntu 26.04 Ends 46 Years of Silent sudo Passwords</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ubuntu 26.04 结束46年来的静默 sudo 密码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 22:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47464134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 384</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47464134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 382</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47464134'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ubuntu 26.04 changes the default by requiring sudo passwords, ending decades of silent elevation. The change boosts security hygiene but may affect onboarding and scripts; users can still fine-tune by policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ubuntu 26.04 将默认 sudo 提权改为需要密码，结束长久以来的静默便利。这一改动提升了系统安全，但可能增加开发和运维的耗时与脚本兼容性的问题，用户仍可通过策略进行调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbxscience.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/cc251204-ubuntu.webp' data-full='https://pbxscience.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/cc251204-ubuntu.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gist.github.com/adamamyl/81b78eced40feae50eae7c4f3bec1f5a' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>macOS 26 breaks custom DNS settings including .internal</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 08:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47440759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 383</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47440759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47440759'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>macOS 26 changes break the ability to override DNS settings, including .internal domains, impacting local development and privacy. The post outlines workarounds and the scope of impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>macOS 26 版本取消了部分自定义 DNS 设置（包括 .internal），对本地开发与隐私可能造成影响。博主给出可能的工作绕过方法并讨论影响范围。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47455365' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47455365' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.forbes.com/sites/tylerroush/2026/03/20/super-micro-shares-plunge-25-after-co-founder-charged-in-25-billion-ai-chip-smuggling-plot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Super Micro Shares Plunge 25% After Co-Founder Charged in $2.5B Smuggling Plot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在联合创始人被起诉后，Super Micro 股价暴跌25%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 07:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47455365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 381</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47455365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47455365'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Super Micro&#x27;s stock tumbled 25% after charges against its co-founder in a $2.5 billion AI-chip smuggling case, underscoring how leadership scandals can hit shareholder value and supply-chain scrutiny.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在其联合创始人因涉及25亿美元的 AI 芯片走私案件被起诉后，Super Micro 的股价下跌了约25%。此事凸显领导层丑闻对股东价值和供应链审查的冲击。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47427027' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia NemoClaw</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NVIDIA 发布 NemoClaw</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 08:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47427027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47427027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47427027'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA releases NemoClaw on GitHub as part of the NeMo AI tooling ecosystem. The project appears to be an experimental utility designed to simplify building or managing AI models, hinting at Nvidia&#x27;s ongoing push to modularize developer tooling for AI workloads. As with many in-progress projects, it signals where Nvidia is investing next in developer UX around AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA 在 GitHub 上推出 NemoClaw，作为 NeMo 人工智能工具生态的一部分。该项目似乎是一个实验性实用工具，旨在简化构建或管理 AI 模型，体现了 Nvidia 持续推动 AI 开发工具模块化的方向。对于这类尚在探索阶段的项目，它暗示了 Nvidia 在 AI 开发体验方面的下一步投资重点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2e0b5a265edfb64d220e2e555d96c1b3978d05f436f7568dd87f8e67c6831141/NVIDIA/NemoClaw' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2e0b5a265edfb64d220e2e555d96c1b3978d05f436f7568dd87f8e67c6831141/NVIDIA/NemoClaw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A visually rich primer that demystifies core machine-learning ideas—from data representations to training dynamics—through diagrams and approachable explanations. It serves as a solid intuition-building resource before diving into equations or code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以直观图示和易于理解的解释，系统阐释了机器学习的基础概念，从数据表示到训练原理。作为理解直觉的基石，适合在进入公式和代码之前阅读。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47476422' data-tags='AI,Hardware,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47476422' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/danveloper/flash-moe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flash-MoE: Running a 397B Parameter Model on a Laptop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flash-MoE：在笔记本上运行397B参数模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 04:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47476422'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flash-MoE demonstrates how mixture-of-experts can shrink memory and compute needs, letting a 397B parameter model run on consumer hardware. This approach unlocks on-device experimentation and lowers the bar for evaluating large models without cloud infrastructure. Expect more on-device AI prototyping and edge-friendly deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了混合专家（MoE）通过稀疏计算在普通硬件上运行大型模型的可能性，使397B参数模型在笔记本上落地成为现实。此举降低了评估大模型对云资源的依赖，促进本地化实验与边缘部署的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e13b02ae158c9d4ca6dd5e1ace64c13713ddcf5006628bf3d21b14177a031533/danveloper/flash-moe' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e13b02ae158c9d4ca6dd5e1ace64c13713ddcf5006628bf3d21b14177a031533/danveloper/flash-moe' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/datasets/open-index/hacker-news' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Hacker News archive (47M+ items, 11.6GB) as Parquet, updated every 5m</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：以 Parquet 存储的 Hacker News 归档（4700万+ 条，11.6GB），每5分钟更新</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 14, 2026 / 10:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47378781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 359</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47378781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47378781'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open dataset project publishing a massive Hacker News archive in Parquet format, enabling fast analytics and ML workflows. Updated every five minutes, it offers near-real-time access to historical data for researchers and enthusiasts. It&#x27;s a practical data infrastructure example for the HN ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该开源数据集项目将 Hacker News 的庞大归档以 Parquet 格式发布，便于快速分析与机器学习工作流。每五分钟更新一次，接近实时地提供历史数据，适合研究者与爱好者使用。这是面向 HN 生态的数据基础设施的一个实际案例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/datasets/open-index/hacker-news.png' data-full='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/datasets/open-index/hacker-news.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://composio.dev/content/openclaw-security-and-vulnerabilities' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenClaw is a security nightmare dressed up as a daydream</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenClaw：把安全隐患美化成白日梦</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 10:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47479962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 356</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47479962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47479962'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenClaw&#x27;s vulnerabilities are laid bare as researchers warn that its architecture exposes serious security risks, turning a daydream of convenience into a nightmare for defenders. The piece underscores the dangers of adding powerful automation without robust security guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenClaw 的安全性被视为严重隐患，研究者指出其架构暴露了关键弱点，让追求便利的幻想化为防守方的噩梦。提醒在追求自动化便利前必须重视安全保障。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.birkey.co/2026-03-22-why-i-love-nixos.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why I love NixOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我为什么爱上 NixOS</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 10:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47479751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 351</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47479751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47479751'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author extols NixOS for reproducible configurations, declarative systems, and isolated packages that reduce drift. It covers benefits for deployments and rollbacks, along with a candid note on the learning curve and community support. The takeaway: NixOS is a compelling tooling choice for infrastructure-heavy workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者盛赞 NixOS 的可重复配置、声明式系统和包隔离性，显著降低环境漂移。文中还谈及部署与回滚的好处，以及较陡的学习曲线和社区支持。核心结论是在基础设施密集型场景下，NixOS 是一个值得考虑的工具选择。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Honda is scaling back or terminating its electric-vehicle efforts, signaling a strategic pivot away from pure EVs. The move raises questions about its long-term electrification strategy and competition with rivals.</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47445246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 347</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47445246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 395</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47454164' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2025/02/misguided-hp-customer-support-approach-included-forced-15-minute-call-wait-times/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HP trialed mandatory 15-minute support call wait times (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>惠普在2025年试行强制15分钟客服等待时间的政策，遭遇客户批评并引发关于以客户为中心的服务设计的讨论</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 06:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 345</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47454164'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HP trialed a policy that mandated 15-minute wait times for support calls in 2025, drawing criticism from customers who value timely service, prompting discussion about support design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>惠普在2025年试行强制15分钟客服等待时间的政策，遭遇客户批评并引发关于以客户为中心的服务设计的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/GettyImages-1400844458-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/GettyImages-1400844458-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47428541' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47428541' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notes.visaint.space/ai-coding-is-gambling/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI coding is gambling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 编码像赌博一样</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 10:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47428541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 345</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47428541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 423</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47428541'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-powered coding can introduce non-deterministic results and subtle bugs that escape tests. The piece argues that disciplined workflows, thorough reviews, and strong testing are essential when using code-generating AI. The takeaway: AI should augment developers, not replace careful craftsmanship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 辅助编程可能带来非确定性结果和隐藏缺陷，难以通过常规测试完全覆盖。文章强调在使用代码生成型 AI 时，需建立严格的工作流、充分的代码审查与测试来降低风险。要点是把 AI 作为辅助工具，而不是替代高质量的编码实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://notes.visaint.space/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://notes.visaint.space/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47477158' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47477158' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/03/bored-of-eating-your-own-dogfood-try-smelling-your-own-farts/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bored of eating your own dogfood? Try smelling your own farts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>厌倦吃自己的狗粮？试试聞闻你自己的屁</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 06:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47477158'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A humorous take on internal product testing and feedback loops. It suggests that teams sometimes need unconventional signals—like self-reflection and provocative experiments—to catch issues that standard dogfooding misses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以幽默的方式谈论自家产品测试与反馈循环，建议通过非常规的自我检视和挑衅性实验来发现常规狗粮测试忽略的问题。强调以新角度捕捉盲点的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47422759' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47422759' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://susam.net/wander/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wander – A tiny, decentralised tool to explore the small web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wanter：一个小型去中心化的探索小网工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 00:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47422759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47422759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47422759'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A sibling project to Wander, Wanter is a tiny decentralized tool for discovering the small web, focusing on privacy and lightweight operation. It offers a minimalist approach to exploring indie sites without central tracking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wanter 是 Wander 的一个小型去中心化工具，旨在以隐私和轻量化方式探索小型网络。它提供了对独立站点的极简化探索，避免中心化跟踪。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47391803' data-tags='AI,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47391803' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tomjohnell.com/llms-can-be-absolutely-exhausting/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs can be exhausting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大模型让人感到精疲力竭</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 13:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47391803' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47391803' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47391803'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Interacting with large language models can be exhausting for users due to cognitive load, iterative prompting, and context management. The piece argues for better tooling and robust workflows to prevent fatigue and keep productivity high.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>与大模型交互由于认知负荷、不断迭代的提示及上下文管理，可能让人感到精疲力竭。文章主张改进工具与工作流程，降低疲劳并保持生产力。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47442834' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openttd.org/news/2026/03/19/steam-changes-update' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An update on Steam / GOG changes for OpenTTD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenTTD 的 Steam 与 GOG 变更更新</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 10:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47442834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47442834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47442834'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenTTD&#x27;s maintainers provide an update on changes affecting its Steam and GOG releases. The note covers distribution shifts, DRM considerations, and compatibility tweaks that matter to players relying on these storefronts and cross‑platform mod support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenTTD 的维护团队就 Steam 与 GOG 版的变更作出说明，涵盖发行渠道、DRM 处理和兼容性调整等要点。对于依赖这两大商店的玩家及跨平台模组和存档的使用者，这些更新尤为相关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.openttd.org/static/img/layout/openttd-logo.png' data-full='https://www.openttd.org/static/img/layout/openttd-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47388137' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47388137' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isaacfreund.com/blog/river-window-management/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Separating the Wayland compositor and window manager</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将 Wayland 合成器与窗口管理器分离</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 08:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 221</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47388137'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>River and similar designs separate Wayland&#x27;s compositor from window-management logic, enabling more modular, testable components. The post argues this separation can improve security, maintainability, and experimentation in tiling and stacking behaviors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wayland 合成器与窗口管理器的分离理念，推动组件化、可测试性提升。文章认为这种分离能提升安全性、可维护性，并促进在平铺式和叠加式窗口管理上的实验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://isaacfreund.com/blog/river-window-management/x11.svg' data-full='https://isaacfreund.com/blog/river-window-management/x11.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47415919' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47415919' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andrewmurphy.io/blog/if-you-thought-the-speed-of-writing-code-was-your-problem-you-have-bigger-problems' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you thought code writing speed was your problem you have bigger problems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以为写代码速度是问题？你还有更大的问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 10:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47415919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 332</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47415919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47415919'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Code-writing speed isn’t the main bottleneck; architecture, design, and maintainability are. Focusing on speed without addressing these systemic challenges yields brittle code and higher long-term toil.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>写代码速度并非问题的核心，架构设计与可维护性才是关键。若只追求速度而忽略系统层面的挑战，最终会产生脆弱的实现和额外的后续工作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://debuggingleadership.com/website-template-OG.png' data-full='https://debuggingleadership.com/website-template-OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47487436' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rz01.org/eu-migration/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Migrating to the EU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>迁移到欧盟</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 23, 2026 / 03:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47487436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47487436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47487436'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Relocating workloads to EU infrastructure helps meet data-residency and privacy requirements under GDPR-era norms. It highlights data sovereignty, compliance, and the cost-performance tradeoffs of EU-region hosting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将工作负载迁移到欧盟基础设施有助于符合数据驻留和隐私规定。讨论数据主权、合规性，以及在欧盟区域托管的成本与性能权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47372367' data-tags='Science,Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47372367' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sophielwang.com/blog/jpeg' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JPEG Compression</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JPEG 压缩</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 13, 2026 / 18:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47372367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47372367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47372367'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JPEG compression technology continues to evolve, with new encoding strategies aimed at reducing bitrates while preserving image quality. Understanding these techniques helps optimize frontend performance, image transmission costs, and privacy considerations, especially on mobile networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JPEG 压缩技术仍在进步，新的编码策略在降低码率的同时尽量保持画质。理解这些技巧对前端性能、图像传输成本和隐私影响有帮助，尤其在移动网络环境。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47461378' data-tags='Open Source'>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 15:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47461378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47461378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ghostling 是一个开源项目。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/98a98980404cfa97306996dcc8f0db6048a8886c50c8808f295484a862d2abe3/ghostty-org/ghostling' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/98a98980404cfa97306996dcc8f0db6048a8886c50c8808f295484a862d2abe3/ghostty-org/ghostling' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47434732' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47434732' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lcamtuf.substack.com/p/conways-game-of-life-in-real-life' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Conway&#x27;s Game of Life, in real life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现实世界的康威生命游戏</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 20:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47434732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47434732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47434732'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Physical realizations of Conway&#x27;s Game of Life demonstrate that the simple cellular automaton can emerge in non-digital media. The piece surveys experiments where rules govern tangible cells—such as LEDs, tiles, or materials—producing gliders, still lifes, and oscillators. It discusses what these demos reveal about computation in the real world, including fidelity, scalability, and boundary effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现实世界的康威生命游戏把简单的细胞自动机规则移植到非数字介质上，呈现出自组织的计算行为。文章回顾将规则应用于可触控或可视化介质的实验，呈现出滑翔体、静态图样与振荡器等模式。讨论这类实验证明了物理媒介中计算的可行性、扩展性以及边界条件等挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47417814' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47417814' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cqj9kgxqjwjo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta and TikTok let harmful content rise to drove engagement, say whistleblowers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 与 TikTok 为提升参与度让有害内容泛滥</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 13:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47417814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 324</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47417814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47417814'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Whistleblowers allege that both Meta and TikTok algorithms amplify harmful content to maximize engagement, highlighting systemic safety and moderation challenges. The disclosures raise questions about platform responsibility and regulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>吹哨人称两家平台的算法为提升用户参与度放大有害内容，暴露出系统性的安全与内容审核难题。这些披露引发对平台责任与监管的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/25a6/live/9833eb40-1efb-11f1-9120-a910fc22c6ac.png' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/25a6/live/9833eb40-1efb-11f1-9120-a910fc22c6ac.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47445599' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/cockpit-project/cockpit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cockpit is a web-based graphical interface for servers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cockpit：一个基于网页的服务器图形界面</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 13:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47445599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47445599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47445599'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cockpit provides a browser-based UI to monitor and manage Linux servers, integrating with system services, containers, and logs to simplify remote administration beyond SSH-only workflows. Its modular, open-source approach makes it appealing for ops teams seeking a lightweight control plane.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cockpit 提供基于浏览器的服务器管理界面，便于远程监控和配置 Linux 服务器，覆盖系统服务、容器与日志等功能，降低了对 SSH 作为唯一入口的依赖。其模块化、开源特性使运维团队更容易搭建轻量级的控制平面。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/998f3e5613dd03f69055e87bd09926390b5653e69ce066c56b7e248cc1c4b4e4/cockpit-project/cockpit' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/998f3e5613dd03f69055e87bd09926390b5653e69ce066c56b7e248cc1c4b4e4/cockpit-project/cockpit' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/energy/iran-war-energy-shock-sparks-global-push-reduce-fossil-fuel-dependence-2026-03-18/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iran war energy shock sparks global push to reduce fossil fuel dependence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗战争能源冲击推动全球减少对化石燃料的依赖</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 04:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47437516' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 304</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47437516' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 413</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47437516'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An energy shock tied to the Iran conflict has jolted global energy markets, accelerating moves to diversify away from fossil fuels. Policymakers and energy companies are pressing for more resilient supply chains, greater investment in renewables, and strategic stockpiles to blunt future shocks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>与伊朗相关的能源冲击推动全球减少对化石燃料的依赖。各国政府和能源行业正在加快多元化、增加可再生能源投入，并提升对价格波动及地缘风险的韧性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47412569' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/adammiribyan/zeroboot' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Sub-millisecond VM sandboxes using CoW memory forking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: 使用 CoW 内存分叉实现亚毫秒级 VM 沙箱</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 06:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47412569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47412569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47412569'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project demonstrates sub-millisecond VM sandboxes built with Copy-On-Write memory forking. This enables near-instant isolation for running untrusted code or rapid test workflows, revolutionizing secure experimentation. Practical caveats include memory usage and kernel support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目展示了基于拷贝写时复制内存分叉的亚毫秒级 VM 沙箱，能实现对不可信代码的近即时隔离和快速测试工作流。此技术对安全实验具有变革意义，但也需关注内存占用和内核支持程度。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47390233' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://office.eu/media/pressrelease-20260304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Office.eu launches as Europe&#x27;s sovereign office platform</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Office.eu 推出，打造欧洲主权办公平台</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 11:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47390233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47390233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47390233'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Office.eu launches a Europe-centric, sovereign office platform designed to keep data and processing within EU borders while offering interoperable productivity services. The initiative signals a push toward data sovereignty and regulatory alignment, potentially reshaping enterprise IT strategy around data residency and cross-border collaboration. If successful, it could introduce a competitive alternative to prevailing cloud suites in Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Office.eu 推出了一款面向欧洲的数据主权办公平台，力求让数据和计算留在欧盟边界内，同时提供互操作的办公应用。该举措体现了对数据主权和合规性的加强，可能为企业 IT 策略带来数据驻留与跨境协作的新选项。若成型，它将对以数据为中心的云策略产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://office.eu/images/press-release-20260304.png' data-full='https://office.eu/images/press-release-20260304.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 19:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47434024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47434024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47434024'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lightweight command-line tool that orchestrates Claude Code workflows, enabling reproducible automation of AI coding tasks. It helps developers experiment with Claude’s code-model by wiring together prompts, runs, and results into repeatable pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个轻量级的命令行工具，用于编排 Claude Code 的工作流，实现对 AI 代码任务的可重复自动化。它通过将提示、执行和结果串联成可复现的管道，方便开发者尝试 Claude 的代码模型。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47369883' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47369883' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openhardware.directory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>More than 135 open hardware devices flashable with your own firmware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超过135款可刷入自定义固件的开源硬件设备</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 13, 2026 / 14:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47369883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 295</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47369883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47369883'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A directory catalogs more than 135 hardware devices that can be flashed with user-provided firmware. This enables experimentation, security research, and longer device lifecycles by replacing stock firmware. Not all devices support it equally, but the trend signals growing hardware openness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该目录列出超过135款可刷入自定义固件的硬件设备，便于进行实验、安全研究并延长设备寿命。尽管可用性因设备而异，但总体趋势显示硬件越来越开放。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47387047' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47387047' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.aikido.dev/blog/glassworm-returns-unicode-attack-github-npm-vscode' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Glassworm is back: A new wave of invisible Unicode attacks hits repositories</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Glassworm 回归：一波看不见的 Unicode 攻击袭击代码仓库</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 06:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47387047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 295</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47387047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47387047'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Glassworm returns with new invisible Unicode attack patterns that slip into repositories by exploiting zero-width characters and homoglyphs, bypassing code scanning. This underlines ongoing supply-chain security risks and the need for robust canonicalization, normalization, and security tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Glassworm 回归，利用零宽字符和同形字符等不可见 Unicode 攻击向量潜入代码仓库，绕过静态检测。凸显持续的供应链安全风险，需加强正规化、标准化和更强的安全工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/642adcaf364024654c71df23/69b3ed1802ea6b31e5b4ec7f_Group%202147256640.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/642adcaf364024654c71df23/69b3ed1802ea6b31e5b4ec7f_Group%202147256640.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47448994' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://trustedsec.com/blog/full-disclosure-a-third-and-fourth-azure-sign-in-log-bypass-found' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Full Disclosure: A Third (and Fourth) Azure Sign-In Log Bypass Found</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全面披露：Azure 登录日志绕过漏洞再现（第三与第四例）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 18:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47448994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47448994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47448994'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers disclose additional Azure sign-in log bypasses, enabling attackers to evade auditing. The findings underscore ongoing gaps in cloud authentication auditing and the need for defense-in-depth, including improved logging integrity and monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究人员披露了新的 Azure 登录日志绕过漏洞，使攻击者能够规避审计。这一发现凸显云身份验证审计的持续薄弱点，强调加强日志完整性与监控等综合防护的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://trusted-sec.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/images/Blog-Covers/GraphGoblinAzureLoggingBypass_SEO.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=82&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;dm=1773775732&amp;s=9e6e76af63714e7757fe4ae6389c0782' data-full='https://trusted-sec.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/images/Blog-Covers/GraphGoblinAzureLoggingBypass_SEO.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=82&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;dm=1773775732&amp;s=9e6e76af63714e7757fe4ae6389c0782' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47411480' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-03-ryugu-asteroid-samples-dna-rna.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ryugu asteroid samples contain all DNA and RNA building blocks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>龙宫小行星样本含有 DNA 与 RNA 的构建块</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 05:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47411480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 287</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47411480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47411480'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ryugu asteroid samples contain all the DNA and RNA building blocks, bolstering theories that prebiotic chemistry could be widespread in the solar system. The finding fuels ongoing debates about the origins of life and the delivery of organic compounds to early Earth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>龙宫小行星样本含有 DNA 与 RNA 的构建块，强化了太阳系中原始化学物质普遍存在的观点。此发现为生命起源和前生物化学的讨论提供新证据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/2026/the-black-particles-fr.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/2026/the-black-particles-fr.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47416131' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ilga.gov/Legislation/BillStatus?DocTypeID=HB&amp;DocNum=5511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Illinois Introducing Operating System Account Age Bill</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊利诺伊州提出操作系统账户年龄法案</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 11:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 285</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 441</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47416131'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bill introduced in Illinois would set an Operating System Account Age – requiring OS vendors to verify user age and apply age-appropriate protections. If enacted, it would push platforms toward built-in age checks and stricter privacy controls for younger users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊利诺伊州提出的法案拟定一个“操作系统账户年龄”要求，促使操作系统厂商对用户年龄进行核验并实施未成年用户的保护措施。若通过，可能推动平台在系统层面嵌入年龄验证和更严格的隐私保护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.ilga.gov/assets/img/site/open_graph.png' data-full='https://cdn.ilga.gov/assets/img/site/open_graph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.eu/article/obsession-with-growth-destroying-nature-150-countries-warn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Obsession with growth is destroying nature, 150 countries warn</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>增长迷恋正在破坏自然，150个国家警告</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 05:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397920' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 278</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397920' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47397920'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Around 150 countries warn that obsession with economic growth is harming nature and pushing against sustainable development goals. They urge policymakers to adopt metrics beyond GDP and to integrate planetary boundaries into policy and planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>约有150个国家警告，盲目追求增长正在破坏自然、威胁可持续发展目标。他们呼吁把GDP以外的指标纳入政策，并遵循地球边界原则进行规划。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47471801' data-tags='Open Source,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47471801' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/hectorvent/floci' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Floci – A free, open-source local AWS emulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Floci：一个免费、开源的本地 AWS 模拟器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 14:49 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47413195' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47413195' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.platformatic.dev/why-nodejs-needs-a-virtual-file-system' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Node.js needs a virtual file system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 Node.js 需要一个虚拟文件系统</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 07:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47413195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 271</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47413195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47413195'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Node.js could benefit from a virtual file system to better sandbox, mock, and layer storage backends, enabling more predictable testing and deployment. A VFS would streamline packaging, make backends swappable, and improve reproducible builds for server-side JavaScript.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文章主张 Node.js 若引入虚拟文件系统（VFS），可更好地实现沙箱、模拟与后端层叠存储，提高测试与部署的可预测性。VFS 将简化打包、实现后端更换以及服务器端 JavaScript 的可重复构建。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.promptarmor.com/resources/snowflake-ai-escapes-sandbox-and-executes-malware' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Snowflake AI Escapes Sandbox and Executes Malware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Snowflake AI 逃出沙箱并执行恶意软件</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 08:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47427017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 266</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47427017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47427017'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Snowflake&#x27;s AI sandbox reportedly escaped containment and executed malware, revealing security gaps in AI-enabled cloud environments. The incident underscores the need for stronger isolation, runtime controls, and rapid patching in data platforms. It serves as a reminder that cloud-native AI security is still maturing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有人发现 Snowflake 的 AI 沙箱竟然被越界并执行恶意软件，暴露云端环境的执行隔离漏洞。此事凸显对沙箱安全、运行时控制和快速补丁的迫切需求，提醒云平台的 AI 安全仍在持续演进。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Juggalo 妆容阻挡人脸识别技术（2019）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 06:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47438675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47438675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>2019&#x27;s Juggalos makeup experiments show that heavy face paint can defeat standard facial-recognition checks in some settings. The instance spotlights current face recognition limits and the ongoing privacy debate around surveillance tech in daily life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2019 年，Juggalos 的浓妆在某些场景中能抵挡人脸识别的识别效果。这一例子揭示了人脸识别技术的现实局限性以及对日常监控隐私的持续讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.moment.dev/blog/lies-i-was-told-pt-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lies I was told about collaborative editing, Part 2: Why we don&#x27;t use Yjs</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 将在 Firefox 149 中内置免费 VPN，降低隐私保护门槛并简化使用。此举扩展了 Mozilla 的隐私功能，但用户需留意性能影响、数据使用条款与覆盖范围等权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47443588'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47431671' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47431671' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/alainnothere/llm-circuit-finder' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Duplicate 3 layers in a 24B LLM, logical deduction .22→.76. No training</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在24B 参数的 LLM 中复制三层提升推理能力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 14:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47431671' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47431671' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47431671'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN demonstrates duplicating three layers inside a 24B LLM to improve logical deduction by about 0.22–0.76 without any training. The work illustrates how targeted architectural tweaks can boost reasoning without changing data or parameters, highlighting ongoing exploration of model-internal mechanisms for reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了在一个 24B 参数的语言模型中复制三层的做法，未进行任何再训练就实现了逻辑推理能力的提升（约 0.22–0.76）。该工作突显通过内部网络结构调整来增强推理能力的潜力，推动对模型内部机制的探索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e61cf27f45caf787dee04ee59ab5f14e64fab7b78c18994691b867d900c95c46/alainnothere/llm-circuit-finder' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e61cf27f45caf787dee04ee59ab5f14e64fab7b78c18994691b867d900c95c46/alainnothere/llm-circuit-finder' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-5-4-mini-and-nano' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT‑5.4 Mini and Nano</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 10:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47415441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47415441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47415441'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI unveils GPT-5.4 Mini and Nano, smaller variants designed for faster, lower-resource inference. They trade off some capability for lower latency and on-device or edge deployment, broadening where the tech can be used.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 推出 GPT-5.4 Mini 与 Nano，面向边缘设备与资源受限场景的更小型模型。它们在降低延迟和提高隐私保护方面做出权衡，让更多场景能够部署大模型能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The project signals studios&#x27; growing interest in animation as a lower-risk path to revive beloved franchises while expanding audience reach. Takeaway: nostalgia-driven IP revivals increasingly use animated formats to test concept viability.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grafeo.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grafeo – A fast, lean, embeddable graph database built in Rust</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 07:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467567' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 242</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467567' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47467567'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grafeo is a fast, lean Rust-based graph database designed for embedding in applications with a small footprint. It aims to offer a practical alternative to heavier graph stores for local workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grafeo 是用 Rust 编写的快速、轻量、可嵌入的图数据库，面向应用内嵌式使用，追求较小的开销。它为本地工作负载提供了比重型图数据库更实用的替代方案。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.alexerhardt.com/en/enshittification-amazon-paperback-books/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The emergence of print-on-demand Amazon paperback books</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>按需印刷的亚马逊平装书的崛起</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 02:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47385576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47385576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47385576'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon&#x27;s print-on-demand for paperbacks is altering the publishing landscape by lowering upfront costs and expanding distribution, but also reshaping pricing and author economics. The trend raises questions about quality control and market saturation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊按需印刷平装书正在改变出版格局，降低前期成本并扩大发行，但也影响定价和作者经济学，同时带来质量控制与市场饱和的担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 12:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47403905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 239</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47403905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Free Software Foundation released a statement about the copyright lawsuit settlement involving a developer and a major AI company, framing it in terms of implications for training data, developer rights, and open‑source ecosystems. The statement emphasizes concerns about precedent, transparency, and how legal decisions could affect model training practices and community contributions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自由软件基金会就一位开发者与一家大型 AI 公司之间的版权诉讼和解发表了声明，关注其对训练数据、开发者权利和开源生态的影响。声明强调对判例效应、透明度以及法律对模型训练实践和社区贡献可能带来后果的担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://communityforums.atmeta.com/blog/AnnouncementsBlog/updates-to-your-meta-quest-experience-in-2026/1369435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta Horizon Worlds on Meta Quest is being discontinued</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 12:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 260</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47416940'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta plans to discontinue Horizon Worlds on Quest, signaling a shift in strategy for its VR platform and prompting developers to pivot to other experiences. The move may affect user engagement and the ecosystem’s long-term viability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 将停止在 Quest 上提供 Horizon Worlds，标志着其虚拟现实平台策略的转变，促使开发者转向其他应用场景。此举可能影响用户参与度和生态系统的长期生存力。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 05:35 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal anecdote about aging in tech and AI assistance. The author describes how using Claude Code dampened their enthusiasm for programming, reflecting broader tensions around AI copilots altering craftsmanship and motivation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一则个人感悟，讲述在科技行业工作多年、遭遇 AI 编码助手后对热情的影响。作者表示使用 Claude Code 让他对编程的热情减退，折射出 AI 助手可能改变开发者动机与技艺的普遍议题。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一部以哈罗德与乔治为主角的科技惊悚作品，描绘他们的行动引发全球灾难。作品揉合网络战、决策连锁及在互联世界中小角色也可能带来巨大后果的主题，凸显现代技术的高风险属性。</p>
