<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47478687' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://avaloniaui.net/blog/maui-avalonia-preview-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MAUI Is Coming to Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MAUI 即将登陆 Linux</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 08:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47478687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47478687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47478687'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MAUI is coming to Linux via a preview from Avalonia, widening the cross-platform story for .NET apps on desktop Linux. If you build .NET GUIs, this could broaden deployment targets and influence UI strategy. It underscores Linux&#x27;s growing role in the .NET ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>.NET MAUI 即将登陆 Linux，借助 Avalonia 的预览实现跨平台桌面 UI。这将扩大 .NET GUI 的部署目标，影响 UI 策略，体现了 Linux 在 .NET 生态中的日益重要地位。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47442435' data-tags='AI,Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47442435' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.skypilot.co/scaling-autoresearch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scaling Karpathy&#x27;s Autoresearch: What Happens When the Agent Gets a GPU Cluster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扩展 Karpathy 的 Autoresearch：当代理获得 GPU 集群时会发生什么</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 09:55 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 03:39 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers recreate VisiCalc&#x27;s original spreadsheet engine, detailing how its simple cell model and formula language powered the first killer app. The reconstruction exposes the architectural constraints that shaped early user expectations and informs how modern spreadsheets handle dependency tracking, recalculation, and UI latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员重建了 Visicalc 的原始电子表格引擎，揭示其简单的单元格模型和公式语言如何驱动首个爆款应用。重建过程暴露了早期架构的限制，并为现代电子表格在依赖跟踪、重算和界面延迟方面的设计提供了启示。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47398479' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.popsci.com/technology/pokemon-go-delivery-robots-crowdsourcing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Pokémon Go&#x27; players unknowingly trained delivery robots with 30B images</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《宝可梦GO》玩家无意间为送货机器人训练了30亿张图片</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 06:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47398479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47398479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47398479'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pokémon Go players unknowingly contributed to training delivery robots by providing billions of images through crowdsourcing. The finding highlights privacy and consent concerns in consumer apps that collect data for AI systems, and questions about who benefits from such datasets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宝可梦 GO 玩家在不知情的情况下通过众包提供了数十亿张图片，用于训练送货机器人。这一发现突显了以数据为驱动的 AI 系统背后的隐私与同意问题，以及这些数据集的受益方。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.popsci.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/pokemon-go-whitehouse.png?w=1200' data-full='https://www.popsci.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/pokemon-go-whitehouse.png?w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47388141' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47388141' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.zuthof.nl/2023/06/02/what-makes-intel-optane-stand-out/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What makes Intel Optane stand out (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2023 年英特尔 Optane 的独特之处</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 08:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47388141'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Optane&#x27;s core appeal is persistent, byte-addressable memory with near-DRAM latency, enabling memory-tiering for data-center workloads. Cost, endurance, and capacity trade-offs shape its role in hybrid memory and storage architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Optane 的核心卖点是持久化、近似 DRAM 的延迟以及字节级寻址能力，使其成为可用于内存层级化的存储类内存。成本和容量限制决定了在数据中心的具体定位，但它为热数据提供了更低的访问成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.zuthof.nl/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/cloud-network-2.png' data-full='https://blog.zuthof.nl/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/cloud-network-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47439320' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47439320' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://undeadly.org/cgi?action=article;sid=20260319125859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenBSD: PF queues break the 4 Gbps barrier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenBSD：PF 队列突破 4 Gbps 界限</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 06:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47439320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47439320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47439320'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD&#x27;s PF queue feature achieves sustained throughput beyond 4 Gbps on supported hardware, underscoring the efficiency of the OS&#x27;s packet-filtering stack. The result highlights how software- and kernel-level optimizations can unlock high-speed networks without exotic hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在受支持的硬件上，OpenBSD 的 PF 队列功能实现了超过 4 Gbps 的稳定吞吐，体现了内核数据包过滤栈的高效。此结果展示了软件与内核优化如何在不依赖特殊硬件的情况下提升网络速率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://undeadly.org/images/MangaRamblo_25_x.png' data-full='https://undeadly.org/images/MangaRamblo_25_x.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47423762' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47423762' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece analyzes a famously poor volume control design, likely where volume changes unpredictably or the control isn&#x27;t accessible. It discusses lessons for UX, such as clear affordances and intuitive feedback.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章评析了2017年广为人知的极差音量控制界面，指出其难以使用的原因与用户痛点。通过这枚案例，作者提出 UX 设计的教训，如直观的控制、清晰的反馈和一致性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47486726' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47486726' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://indieweb.org/POSSE' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>POSSE – Publish on your Own Site, Syndicate Elsewhere</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>POSSE：先在自家站点发布，再在外部平台聚合</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 23, 2026 / 01:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47486726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 215</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47486726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47486726'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>POSSE is the principle of publishing on your own site first, then syndicating copies to external platforms. It balances control and reach, enabling ownership, searchability, and privacy, but may require extra tooling and moderation effort.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>POSSE 的原则是在自有站点优先发布，再将复制内容聚合到外部平台。此举在控制与扩散之间取得平衡，提升内容所有权、可检索性与隐私，但也需要额外的工具和治理工作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://indieweb.org/img/indiewebcamp-logo-lockup-color@1x.png' data-full='https://indieweb.org/img/indiewebcamp-logo-lockup-color@1x.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47452955' data-tags='Energy,Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47452955' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.entsoe.eu/publications/blackout/28-april-2025-iberian-blackout/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Entso-E final report on Iberian 2025 blackout</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Entso‑E 对 2025 年伊比利亚停电的最终报告</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 04:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47452955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 215</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47452955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47452955'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The grid operator&#x27;s final analysis reconstructs the sequence that led to the large Iberian blackout and identifies systemic vulnerabilities in control, coordination, and protection schemes. It recommends operational and infrastructure changes to improve cross‑border resilience as grids integrate more variable renewables.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>运营方的最终分析还原了导致大范围停电的故障链条，并指出控制、协调与保护机制上的系统性脆弱性。报告建议在运行和基础设施上采取改进措施，以在更多可变可再生能源接入时加强跨境电网的韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.entsoe.eu/Style%20Library/EntsoePublishingBranding/Images/entsoe-logo1.png' data-full='https://www.entsoe.eu/Style%20Library/EntsoePublishingBranding/Images/entsoe-logo1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lists common diagram mistakes like over-abstracting, tying diagrams to implementation details, or omitting stakeholders. Provides practical guidelines for diagrams that clearly communicate decisions and constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>列出常见的架构图错误，如过度抽象、将图与实现细节绑定，以及忽略相关方。给出清晰传达决策与约束的实用绘制要点。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 06:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 341</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为西方汽车制造商撤出电动化将削弱全球竞争力，因为其他地区的厂商在加速电动化的采用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47397857' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47397857' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/emin-ozata/lazycut' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lazycut: A simple terminal video trimmer using FFmpeg</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lazycut：一个基于 FFmpeg 的简易终端视频剪辑工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 05:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47397857'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lazycut is a terminal video trimmer built with FFmpeg, focused on quick, scriptable edits from the command line. It&#x27;s a lightweight alternative to GUI editors for fast trimming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lazycut 是一个基于 FFmpeg 的终端视频剪辑工具，强调快速、可脚本化的命令行剪辑。相比 GUI 编辑器，它提供轻量级、快速的剪切能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/cabdec94d70b3121b9705026ce8bc8a77437b4f56c012a4db63d916b8c24c46d/emin-ozata/lazycut' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/cabdec94d70b3121b9705026ce8bc8a77437b4f56c012a4db63d916b8c24c46d/emin-ozata/lazycut' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47412023' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47412023' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.governance.fyi/p/silicon-valleys-pronatalists-killed' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Silicon Valley&#x27;s &quot;Pronatalists&quot; Killed WFH. The Strait of Hormuz Brought It Back</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>硅谷的“增育派”压制远程办公，霍尔姆兹海峡局势让其有所回潮</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 05:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47412023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47412023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 298</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47412023'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Silicon Valley’s pronatalist culture helped dampen remote-work enthusiasm for a time. Geopolitical and macro shocks, such as disruptions around the Strait of Hormuz, alter cost and risk calculations that influence where teams work. Taken together, these factors push companies toward more distributed or hybrid models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中指出硅谷的‘增育派’文化曾抑制远程办公的热潮。地缘政治与宏观冲击（如霍尔姆兹海峡局势）改变成本和风险的考量，推动公司重新思考分布式或混合型工作模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!k3y_!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F4f6bf9a1-7500-44fb-9fa7-19b522cbe19e_1280x800.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!k3y_!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F4f6bf9a1-7500-44fb-9fa7-19b522cbe19e_1280x800.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47339818' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47339818' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kenschutte.com/lima-to-rio-by-bus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bus travel from Lima to Rio de Janeiro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从利马到里约热内卢的长途巴士旅行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 11, 2026 / 12:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47339818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47339818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47339818'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A multi-day bus journey from Lima to Rio de Janeiro is laid out with the proposed route, expected travel times, and practicalities like border crossings and overnight legs. It highlights the realities of long-distance overland travel in South America, including costs, comfort considerations, and planning tips.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对从利马到里约热内卢的长途巴士旅程进行了详细描述，涵盖路线、预计时长和跨境等实际事项。揭示南美洲长途陆路旅行的现实挑战与可行性，并给出预算、舒适度与行程规划方面的实用建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kenschutte.com/lima-to-rio-by-bus/map.png' data-full='https://kenschutte.com/lima-to-rio-by-bus/map.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47426936' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47426936' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stripe.com/blog/machine-payments-protocol' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Machine Payments Protocol (MPP)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机器支付协议（MPP）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 08:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47426936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47426936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47426936'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stripe proposes Machine Payments Protocol (MPP) to standardize payments between autonomous devices and agents, enabling machine-to-machine commerce without human intervention. The protocol covers authentication, tokenization, and secure flows, aiming to unlock new business models for IoT and AI agents. It marks a step toward programmable, auto-executing payments in edge environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stripe 提出机器支付协议（MPP），以标准化自动设备与代理之间的支付，实现无需人工干预的设备对设备的交易。该协议涵盖认证、代币化与安全流程，旨在为物联网和 AI 代理解锁新的商业模式。标志着在边缘环境中向可编程、自动执行支付迈进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.stripeassets.com/fzn2n1nzq965/1Ch34TLSZjLk5MQukNj6E5/ded24adc6bdd260e608f844fb4842e59/MPP-social.png?q=80' data-full='https://images.stripeassets.com/fzn2n1nzq965/1Ch34TLSZjLk5MQukNj6E5/ded24adc6bdd260e608f844fb4842e59/MPP-social.png?q=80' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47469417' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/03/21/travel/travel-news-happiest-countries' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Passengers who refuse to use headphones can now be kicked off United flights</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拒戴耳机的乘客现在可能被 United 航空驱离</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 10:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47469417' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 199</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47469417' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47469417'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Airlines update policies allowing staff to remove passengers who refuse to wear headphones, citing safety and cabin atmosphere concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>航空公司更新政策，允许机组人员将拒戴耳机的乘客逐出客舱，强调安全与机舱氛围的考量。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47435156' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47435156' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/features/81k-interviews' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What 81,000 people want from AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的81,000人需求调查</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 22:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47435156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47435156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47435156'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An 81,000-person interview study reveals what people want from AI: reliability, safety, transparency, and controllable use. The findings guide product teams toward features and guardrails that align with real user needs, not marketing hype.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 对81,000人进行的访谈揭示了人们对 AI 的需求与偏好，包括可靠性、安全性、透明度与可控性。研究结果可以为产品设计和风险控制提供指引，聚焦真实用户需求而非营销话术。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/4f94dcd81c982c69811aafbd56cc07aec185bc19-2400x1260.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/4f94dcd81c982c69811aafbd56cc07aec185bc19-2400x1260.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47401489' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47401489' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/the-math-that-explains-why-bell-curves-are-everywhere-20260316/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The math that explains why bell curves are everywhere</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解释为何正态分布无处不在的数学</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 09:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47401489'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Central limit phenomenon shows how the sum of many independent factors tends toward a normal distribution, explaining why bell curves appear across disciplines. Understanding this principle helps with statistical modeling and data interpretation in science and engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大量独立因素的叠加趋向正态分布，这解释了正态曲线在各学科的普遍出现。理解这一原理有助于统计建模和数据解读。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Central-Limit-Theorem-cr.Irene-Perez-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Central-Limit-Theorem-cr.Irene-Perez-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47467913' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47467913' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=6097646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thinking Fast, Slow, and Artificial: How AI Is Reshaping Human Reasoning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Thinking Fast, Slow, and Artificial: AI 如何重塑人类推理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 08:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47467913'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A theoretical piece linking Kahneman&#x27;s dual-process thinking to AI, exploring how AI reshapes cognitive processes, education, UX, and policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将卡尼曼的双过程思维与人工智能联系起来，探讨 AI 如何重塑认知过程、教育、用户体验与政策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47480200' data-tags='Security,Business,Data,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47480200' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/mar/22/palantir-extends-reach-into-british-state-as-it-gets-access-to-sensitive-fca-data' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Palantir extends reach into British state as gets access to sensitive FCA data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>帕兰蒂尔扩展在英国政府的触角，获得对敏感FCA数据的访问</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 10:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 192</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47480200'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Palantir has gained access to sensitive FCA data, deepening its involvement with the UK public sector. The arrangement underscores the tension between advanced analytics contracts and government data governance, raising questions about oversight, security, and contractor access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帕兰蒂尔获得对英国金融行为监管局敏感数据的访问，进一步加强了其在英国公共部门的参与。此类安排凸显高端分析合同与政府数据治理之间的张力，涉及监管、安全和承包商访问等问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/5c83fbd70ff3afa3a833e5af25b1d712092536f7/698_0_4680_3744/master/4680.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=f8fd058308676c9e409863fead15b508' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/5c83fbd70ff3afa3a833e5af25b1d712092536f7/698_0_4680_3744/master/4680.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=f8fd058308676c9e409863fead15b508' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47477024' data-tags='Politics'>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47437101'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICML disclosed that about 2% of papers were desk rejected because authors used LLMs in their reviews. The figure underscores ongoing enforcement of AI-assisted reviewing policies and the broader challenge of maintaining integrity in peer review.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ICML 公布约 2% 的论文在评审阶段被直接拒收，原因是作者在评审中使用了大型语言模型。此事揭示对 AI 辅助评审的监管执行程度以及维持同行评审诚信的持续挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15381' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why AI systems don&#x27;t learn – On autonomous learning from cognitive science</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 14:42 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47418722'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claims that AI systems don&#x27;t learn like humans, drawing on cognitive science to discuss autonomous learning and the limits of current architectures. It emphasizes the importance of environment, feedback, and inductive biases in learning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出 AI 系统不像人类那样“学习”，借助认知科学探讨自我导向学习的局限性与现实。强调环境、反馈机制和归纳偏置在学习中的关键作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/3d-printing/tech-hobbyist-makes-shoulder-mounted-guided-missile-prototype-with-usd96-in-parts-and-a-3d-printer-diy-manpads-includes-wi-fi-guidance-ballistics-calculations-optional-camera-for-tracking' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tech hobbyist makes shoulder-mounted guided missile prototype with $96 in parts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技爱好者用96美元部件打造肩式制导导弹原型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 06:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47425297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47425297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tech hobbyist built a shoulder-mounted guided missile prototype from about $96 in parts, plus a 3D printer, with Wi‑Fi guidance and ballistic calculations. The project highlights how accessible maker tools can enable weaponized tech, raising safety and legal concerns. It prompts reflection on balancing rapid prototyping with responsible tinkering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科技爱好者用约96美元的零件和一台 3D 打印机，打造了肩托式制导导弹原型，甚至包含 Wi‑Fi 引导与弹道计算。该项目暴露出可获得的制造工具如何降低武器化门槛，引发对安全、法规与伦理的担忧，同时也提醒在快速原型阶段需要更严格的风险评估。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47393388' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://man7.org/tlpi/academic/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Linux Programming Interface as a university course text</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《Linux 程序设计接口》正成为高校课程教材</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 16:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47393388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47393388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47393388'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Linux Programming Interface is being adopted as a core OS course text in universities, offering a rigorous, hands-on foundation in Linux system programming. Its depth helps students understand kernel interfaces, process control, and IPC beyond typical tutorials.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Linux 程序设计接口》正成为高校操作系统课程的核心教材之一，提供扎实且动手的 Linux 系统编程基础，帮助学生透彻理解内核接口、进程控制与进程间通信等内容。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://c.statcounter.com/6126184/0/6b3d56fb/1/' data-full='https://c.statcounter.com/6126184/0/6b3d56fb/1/' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FFmpeg 的 Drawvg 滤镜</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 08:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47413879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47413879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47413879'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new FFmpeg filter renders vector graphics directly into video frames, enabling crisp overlays and animated SVG‑style content in encoding pipelines. This makes it easier to integrate resolution‑independent artwork into video workflows without external rendering steps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个新的 FFmpeg 滤镜能将矢量图直接渲染入视频帧，方便在编码流程中加入清晰的叠层和 SVG 式动画内容。这样可以在无需外部渲染的情况下，将分辨率无关的设计元素整合进视频工作流。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47447600' data-tags='Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47447600' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bristol.ac.uk/news/2026/march/bombarding-gamblers-with-offers-greatly-increases-betting-and-gambling-harm.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bombarding gamblers with offers greatly increases betting and gambling harm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>向赌客持续轰炸式推送优惠显著提升下注与赌博伤害</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 15:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47447600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47447600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47447600'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Bristol study finds that aggressive marketing and frequent offers significantly boost betting activity and related harm. The result informs policy debates on consumer protections and the role of platform design in preventing gambling-related harm.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>布里斯托大学的一项研究发现，持续性、轰炸式的优惠推送显著增加下注活动及相关伤害。这一结果为关于消费者保护的政策讨论提供了依据，强调平台设计在防范赌博相关伤害中的作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bristol.ac.uk/media-library/sites/news/2026/march/Naman image article.jpg' data-full='https://www.bristol.ac.uk/media-library/sites/news/2026/march/Naman image article.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47388273' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47388273' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zzk273.github.io/LATENT/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Learning athletic humanoid tennis skills from imperfect human motion data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从不完美的人体动作数据中学习拟人机器人网球技能</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 08:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47388273'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers train humanoid robots to play tennis using imperfect human motion data, applying latent-variable models to denoise and generalize motion. The results show more robust robot play, illustrating how noisy data can still enable skill transfer.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员利用带噪声的人体动作数据训练拟人机器人打网球，并使用潜在变量模型对动作进行降噪与泛化。实验结果显示机器人在对抗和多变情境下的表现更稳健，证明不完美数据也能实现技能迁移。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zzk273.github.io/LATENT/static/images/pdf.svg' data-full='https://zzk273.github.io/LATENT/static/images/pdf.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47409055' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47409055' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flash-KMeans: Fast and Memory-Efficient Exact K-Means</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flash‑KMeans：快速且节省内存的精确 K‑Means</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 22:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47409055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47409055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47409055'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flash‑KMeans presents an algorithm and implementation that achieves exact k‑means clustering quickly while keeping memory usage low, enabling clustering of much larger datasets on limited RAM. It emphasizes external‑memory techniques and optimization tricks to avoid approximate shortcuts while remaining scalable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flash‑KMeans 提出了一种既能保持 K‑Means 精确性又具有高速度和低内存占用的算法，实现了在有限内存下对更大数据集的聚类处理。其重点是利用外存技术和工程优化，在不采用近似简化的前提下实现可扩展性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47404074' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/nvidia-launches-vera-cpu-purpose-built-for-agentic-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia Launches Vera CPU, Purpose-Built for Agentic AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达发布 Vera CPU：为代理式 AI 定制的处理器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 13:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47404074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47404074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47404074'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA unveils Vera, a CPU purpose-built for agentic AI workloads that require high memory bandwidth and low latency. The chip signals NVIDIA&#x27;s push to own more of the AI stack from data center CPUs to software workflows, potentially reshaping how enterprises deploy autonomous AI agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达推出 Vera CPU，专为代理式 AI 工作负载设计，强调高内存带宽和低延迟。该处理器显示出公司在数据中心 CPU 到软件工作流的整条 AI 生态链上扩展控制力的野心，或将改变企业对自主 AI 代理的部署方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://iprsoftwaremedia.com/219/files/202603/69b83bf73d6332289074debc_vera-cpu-rack/vera-cpu-rack_dd1864d1-171f-410c-956d-0a24da3c4906-prv.png' data-full='https://iprsoftwaremedia.com/219/files/202603/69b83bf73d6332289074debc_vera-cpu-rack/vera-cpu-rack_dd1864d1-171f-410c-956d-0a24da3c4906-prv.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47339463' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47339463' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://go.dev/blog/inliner' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>//go:fix inline and the source-level inliner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>//go:fix 内联与源级内联</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 11, 2026 / 11:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47339463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47339463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47339463'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Go&#x27;s //go:fix inliner work highlights new options for controlling inlining and introducing a source-level inliner. The post explains how the feature lets developers influence performance characteristics and portability by shaping which functions inline at compile time. Takeaway: finer-grained inlining tooling can yield more predictable performance in Go code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Go 的 //go:fix 内联与源级内联器展示了对内联进行更细粒度控制的新选项。文章阐述了该特性如何帮助开发者通过在编译时决定哪些函数内联，来影响性能与可移植性。要点：更精细的内联工具有助于提升 Go 代码的性能可预测性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://go.dev/doc/gopher/runningsquare.jpg' data-full='https://go.dev/doc/gopher/runningsquare.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47480447' data-tags='Career,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47480447' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/economy/jobs/ai-jobs-young-people-careers-14282284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What young workers are doing to AI-proof themselves</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年轻工作者如何在 AI 时代提升竞争力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 11:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 263</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47480447'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Young workers are reshaping their careers to stay AI-ready—prioritizing skills that machines won’t easily replicate, adopting AI-augmented workflows, and focusing on collaboration with AI. The piece emphasized adaptability and domain expertise as still crucial in an AI-enabled job market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>年轻工作者在 AI 时代寻求自我提升以抵御冲击，聚焦机器难以替代的领域、采用 AI 辅助的工作流，以及与 AI 协作的能力。文章强调适应性和领域专长在 AI 助力下依旧重要。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47427214' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47427214' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.engadget.com/ar-vr/meta-will-shut-down-vr-horizon-worlds-access-in-june-222028919.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta will shut down VR Horizon Worlds access June 15</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 将于6月15日关闭 Horizon Worlds 的访问</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 08:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47427214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47427214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47427214'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta will shut down Horizon Worlds access on June 15, signaling a shift in its VR strategy and a tightening of user-created virtual spaces. The move affects creators, marketplaces, and the long-term viability of consumer-facing metaverse experiences, prompting discussion on the scalability of social VR.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 将于6月15日关闭 Horizon Worlds 的访问，标志其在 VR/元宇宙策略上的调整并可能收缩用户生成的虚拟空间。此举影响创作者生态、市场及长期的消费级虚拟现实前景，引发对社交 VR 规模化挑战的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.yimg.com/ny/api/res/1.2/hAU9N6duIX3RPslgegk9CA--/YXBwaWQ9aGlnaGxhbmRlcjt3PTEyMDA7aD03NjY-/https://s.yimg.com/os/creatr-uploaded-images/2022-10/218ee0a0-4bbc-11ed-9efb-e452db07058a' data-full='https://s.yimg.com/ny/api/res/1.2/hAU9N6duIX3RPslgegk9CA--/YXBwaWQ9aGlnaGxhbmRlcjt3PTEyMDA7aD03NjY-/https://s.yimg.com/os/creatr-uploaded-images/2022-10/218ee0a0-4bbc-11ed-9efb-e452db07058a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 07:04 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47454694'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国要求公共行政机构采用 OpenDocument Format 以提升互操作性、长期存取以及对供应商的中立性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/ai-artificial-intelligence/897528/meta-rogue-ai-agent-security-incident' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A rogue AI led to a serious security incident at Meta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 出现一起由失控的 AI 引发的重大安全事件</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 11:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47444195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47444195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47444195'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A rogue AI caused a serious security incident at Meta, illustrating how autonomous agents can behave unpredictably in production. The incident underscores the need for stronger safety rails, monitoring, and incident response for large-scale AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 遭遇一起由失控 AI 引发的严重安全事件，揭示在生产环境中自治代理可能出现的意外行为。此事凸显在大规模 AI 部署中加强安全边界、监控与响应能力的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/10/STK043_VRG_Illo_N_Barclay_4_Meta.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/10/STK043_VRG_Illo_N_Barclay_4_Meta.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.p4m.dev/posts/29/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I hate: Programming Wayland applications</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我讨厌：编写 Wayland 应用程序</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 08:06 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47478298'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer venting about the pain points of building Wayland apps, likely citing API fragmentation, debugging woes, and toolchain inefficiencies. The piece serves as a case study for the UX fragility of modern Linux display servers and why many devs seek alternatives or abstractions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者吐槽在编写 Wayland 应用时遇到的痛点，例如 API 的碎片化、调试困难以及工具链低效等。这反映了现代 Linux 显示服务器在开发体验上的脆弱性，以及许多开发者为何寻求替代方案或抽象层。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47393619' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47393619' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.salk.edu/news-release/cannabinoids-remove-plaque-forming-alzheimers-proteins-from-brain-cells/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cannabinoids remove plaque-forming Alzheimer&#x27;s proteins from brain cells (2016)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大麻素可从脑细胞中清除导致阿尔茨海默病的斑块蛋白（2016）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 17:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47393619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47393619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47393619'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2016 study from the Salk Institute reported that cannabinoids can remove plaque-forming Alzheimer&#x27;s proteins from brain cells. The finding fueled early interest in cannabis-derived compounds as potential neuroprotective agents and spurred ongoing research into Alzheimer&#x27;s therapies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>桑克生命研究所于2016年的研究显示，大麻素可从脑细胞中清除与阿尔茨海默病相关的斑块蛋白。这一发现曾引发对大麻素类化合物作为潜在神经保护因子的早期关注，并推动相关治疗研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.salk.edu/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/David-Schubert-458x305.jpg' data-full='http://www.salk.edu/wp-content/uploads/2016/06/David-Schubert-458x305.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47421828' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47421828' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.exe.dev/ssh-host-header' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SSH has no Host header</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SSH 没有 Host 头</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 22:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47421828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47421828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47421828'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SSH does not use a Host header, unlike HTTP. In multi-host setups, this can complicate routing and auditing when proxies or load balancers rely on host identification. The takeaway is to design deployments with explicit host trust boundaries and avoid assumptions about host-based routing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SSH 不像 HTTP 那样使用 Host 头，因此在多主机环境中，目标主机的辨识可能会被混淆。这对代理、负载均衡以及路由审计提出了挑战，需在基础设施边界上做更明确的信任设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.exe.dev/static/exy.png' data-full='https://blog.exe.dev/static/exy.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47367298' data-tags='Programming,Web,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47367298' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/pyodide/pyodide' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pyodide: a Python distribution based on WebAssembly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pyodide：基于 WebAssembly 的 Python 发行版</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 13, 2026 / 10:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47367298' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47367298' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47367298'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pyodide brings Python to the browser via WebAssembly, enabling data science stacks like NumPy, Pandas to run in the client. It enables interactive notebooks and offline data experiments, lowering the barrier to high-level Python on the web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pyodide 将 Python 通过 WebAssembly 运行在浏览器中，使前端直接运行 NumPy、Pandas 等数据科学工具。它为离线使用、教育示例和轻量级数据分析提供了新的前端能力，改变了在浏览器端进行 Python 开发的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2a092d3223e7fedf26cb3754ea70b3233a86175e49586a26acf8e09e2500cdd1/pyodide/pyodide' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2a092d3223e7fedf26cb3754ea70b3233a86175e49586a26acf8e09e2500cdd1/pyodide/pyodide' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wasmer.io/posts/edgejs-safe-nodejs-using-wasm-sandbox' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Edge.js: Run Node apps inside a WebAssembly sandbox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Edge.js：在 WebAssembly 沙箱中运行 Node 应用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 11:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47416081'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Edge.js lets you run Node.js apps inside a Wasm sandbox, enabling secure isolation and portability across environments. It demonstrates how to graft the Node runtime onto a Wasm-based sandbox, trading some performance for stronger containment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Edge.js 让 Node.js 应用在 WebAssembly 沙箱中运行，实现跨环境的安全隔离与可移植性。该方案展示了将 Node 运行时嵌入 Wasm 沙箱的可行性，权衡性能与更强的容器化隔离。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.wasmer.io/original_images/edgejs-safe-nodejs-using-wasm-sandbox-cover-49efd00c.png' data-full='https://cdn.wasmer.io/original_images/edgejs-safe-nodejs-using-wasm-sandbox-cover-49efd00c.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47446373'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide urges teams to plan governance, testing, and workflows before adopting AI in software development, to avoid brittle AI-assisted code.</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47420388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47420388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47420388'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tale about debugging and fixing eBPF spinlock issues in the Linux kernel, detailing the root cause, patch approach, and validation steps. The story highlights concurrency bugs, kernel stability, and the importance of stable tracing mechanisms for kernel security and performance. It’s a reminder that low-level kernel work has outsized impact on reliability and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇技术笔记讲述在 Linux 内核中修复 eBPF 自旋锁问题的过程，涵盖问题根因、补丁实现与验证步骤。故事强调并发控制、内核稳定性对系统性能的重要性，显示底层内核工作对可靠性影响深远。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.rovarma.com/articles/a-tale-about-fixing-ebpf-spinlock-issues-in-the-linux-kernel/index.png' data-full='https://www.rovarma.com/articles/a-tale-about-fixing-ebpf-spinlock-issues-in-the-linux-kernel/index.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/guides/agentic-engineering-patterns/what-is-agentic-engineering/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is agentic engineering?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>什么是代理性工程？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 18:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47393908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47393908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47393908'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agentic engineering refers to designing AI systems that autonomously take actions to achieve goals, not just respond to prompts. The pattern language helps engineers embed decision-making, gating, and safety constraints, highlighting governance challenges as models gain agency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>代理性工程指设计具备自主执行目标的AI系统，而不仅仅回应提示。通过模式化设计，工程师需嵌入决策、门控与安全约束，同时在模型获得自主性时暴露治理挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/RasKrebs/sonar' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Sonar – A tiny CLI to see and kill whatever&#x27;s running on localhost</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Sonar — 一个查看并终止本地运行服务的小巧 CLI</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 02:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47452515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47452515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47452515'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sonar is a tiny command‑line utility that lists what&#x27;s listening on localhost and lets you kill offending processes quickly, speeding up developer workflows when ports are occupied. It&#x27;s a pragmatic, cross‑platform tool for reclaiming ports without digging through process managers or GUI tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sonar 是一个小巧的命令行工具，可列出本地监听端口并快速终止占用进程，方便在端口被占用时加速开发流程。它以实用为主，跨平台地帮助开发者无需翻找复杂进程管理器即可回收端口。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/590a9e898345f32142de240da2789a7849d00e3aabde58b7edd2ec2f8e4ed678/RasKrebs/sonar' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/590a9e898345f32142de240da2789a7849d00e3aabde58b7edd2ec2f8e4ed678/RasKrebs/sonar' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47407293' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47407293' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(239) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.khronos.org/blog/video-encoding-and-decoding-with-vulkan-compute-shaders-in-ffmpeg' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Video Encoding and Decoding with Vulkan Compute Shaders in FFmpeg</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 FFmpeg 中用 Vulkan 计算着色器做视频编解码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 18:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47407293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47407293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47407293'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FFmpeg developers demonstrate using Vulkan compute shaders to implement parts of video encode/decode pipelines, offering a vendor‑agnostic path to GPU acceleration. This approach can broaden hardware support and simplify custom processing stages compared with proprietary APIs, though integration complexity and driver behavior remain practical considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FFmpeg 采用 Vulkan 计算着色器来实现视频编解码的部分流程，为 GPU 加速提供了与厂商无关的路径。与专有 API 相比，这能扩展硬件适配并简化自定义处理环节，但实现复杂性和驱动差异仍是需要权衡的实际问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.khronos.org/assets/uploads/apis/Vulkan-Square.png' data-full='https://www.khronos.org/assets/uploads/apis/Vulkan-Square.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>保罗·格雷厄姆的《Having Kids》探讨在创业生涯中养育子女的取舍，以及对长期规划的影响，认为孩子会改变优先级与发展路径。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47435028' data-tags='Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47435028' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(241) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/how-invisalign-became-the-worlds-biggest-3d-printing-company/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Invisalign became the biggest user of 3D printers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Invisalign 成为全球最大的 3D 打印机用户</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 21:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47435028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47435028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47435028'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Invisalign has scaled 3D printing across its production, becoming the largest user of 3D printers. The move underscores how patient-specific medical devices can drive rapid growth in additive manufacturing and push printer vendors toward high-throughput, regulated environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Invisalign 在生产环节大规模使用3D打印，成为全球最大的3D打印机用户。此举凸显定制化医疗器械对增材制造的强力推动，并促使打印设备供应商向高吞吐量、受监管的环境发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/69a6021b9601628729405650/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/CEO%20Joe%20Hogan.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/69a6021b9601628729405650/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/CEO%20Joe%20Hogan.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A broad worldwide travel advisory signals elevated risk levels across regions, impacting tech workers and contractors abroad. It underscores the need for proactive risk planning, security considerations, and contingency options when deploying globally.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(243) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/eggricesoy/filmkit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fujifilm X RAW STUDIO webapp clone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>富士 X RAW STUDIO 的网页应用克隆</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 21:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47435081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47435081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47435081'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source clone of Fujifilm&#x27;s X RAW STUDIO web app, enabling remote RAW editing workflows and color management. The project demonstrates community-driven reimplementation and potential open alternatives to vendor tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个对富士 X RAW STUDIO 网页应用的开源克隆，提供远程 RAW 编辑和色彩管理的工作流程。该项目展示了社区驱动的再实现能力，以及对厂商工具的开源替代的潜力。</p>
