<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47364260' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47364260' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(244) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mr-fatalyst/oxyde' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Oxyde – Pydantic-native async ORM with a Rust core</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Oxyde——基于 Pydantic 的原生异步 ORM，核心为 Rust</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 13, 2026 / 06:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47364260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47364260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47364260'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oxyde is a Python ORM that uses Pydantic for data validation and typing, with a Rust core delivering performance and safety. It aims to offer a modern, async-first ORM experience that competes with SQLAlchemy-style tooling, potentially changing how Python apps model and query data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Oxyde 是一个 Python ORM，基于 Pydantic 进行数据校验和类型定义，核心用 Rust 实现以提升性能和安全性。它强调异步优先的使用体验，可能对现有的 SQLAlchemy 等工具形成竞争，并改变 Python 应用的数据建模与查询方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/75ecf54e87dfe5b55ccac42731ff53421e91ebc3db6dafa7b8488844736b275f/mr-fatalyst/oxyde' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/75ecf54e87dfe5b55ccac42731ff53421e91ebc3db6dafa7b8488844736b275f/mr-fatalyst/oxyde' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47471271' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47471271' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nesbitt.io/2026/03/21/how-to-attract-ai-bots-to-your-open-source-project.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to attract AI bots to your open source project</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何吸引 AI 代理加入你的开源项目</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 13:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47471271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47471271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47471271'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide on attracting AI bots to open source projects, covering API design, documentation, testability, and governance that make codebases scan-friendly for agents. It highlights the growing intersection of OSS and AI tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>给开源项目吸引 AI 代理的实用指南，强调设计友好 API、完善文档、良好的测试性与治理结构等要素，以便 AI 代理可以更容易扫描和参与代码库。凸显 OSS 与 AI 工具日益交叉的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nesbitt.io/images/boxes.png' data-full='https://nesbitt.io/images/boxes.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47401734' data-tags='AI,Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47401734' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Speed at the cost of quality: Study of use of Cursor AI in open source projects (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以速度换取质量：Cursor AI 在开源项目中的应用研究（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 10:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47401734'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study finds that Cursor AI speeds up development but can degrade code quality and maintainability in open-source projects. It recommends balancing automation with thorough reviews, tests, and architectural discipline to avoid accumulating technical debt.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现 Cursor AI 能提高开源项目的开发速度，但往往降低代码质量与可维护性。建议在追求自动化的同时加强评审、测试和架构纪律，以避免积累技术债。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47418247' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47418247' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mdp.github.io/regex-blaster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Regex Blaster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Regex Blaster</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 14:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47418247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47418247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47418247'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tool named Regex Blaster for stress‑testing regular expressions to reveal performance problems like catastrophic backtracking and denial‑of‑service vulnerabilities. It helps developers find pathological inputs and optimize patterns to avoid slowdowns in production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>名为 Regex Blaster 的工具用于对正则表达式进行压力测试，揭示灾难性回溯等性能问题与可能的服务拒绝风险。它帮助开发者找出存在问题的输入并优化模式以避免上线时的性能崩溃。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47364337' data-tags='Hardware,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47364337' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://randomascii.wordpress.com/2018/01/07/finding-a-cpu-design-bug-in-the-xbox-360/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Finding a CPU Design Bug in the Xbox 360 (2018)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Xbox 360 中发现的 CPU 设计漏洞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 13, 2026 / 06:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47364337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47364337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47364337'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A veteran hardware researcher uncovered a CPU design bug in the Xbox 360, illustrating how subtle architectural flaws can persist across generations. The finding underscores the importance of deep hardware verification and may influence future console and embedded CPU testing practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>资深硬件研究者在 Xbox 360 中发现了一个 CPU 设计缺陷，展示了架构层面的微妙错误如何在多年后仍然存在。此发现强调了深入的硬件验证的重要性，可能影响未来控制台和嵌入式 CPU 的测试方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://randomascii.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/01/xbox360_processor_die-fixed_thumb.jpg' data-full='https://randomascii.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/01/xbox360_processor_die-fixed_thumb.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47466294' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/how-byds-ev-charger-got-even-faster-and-it-might-not-matter-as-much-as-you-think/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How BYD got EV chargers to work almost as fast as gas pumps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BYD 如何让电动车充电速度快得几乎等同于加油</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 05:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47466294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47466294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47466294'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BYD&#x27;s charging innovations push EV charging speeds toward parity with gasoline fueling, highlighting hardware, software, and network optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>比亚迪通过在硬件、软件与网络上的创新，将充电速度提升至与加油接近的水平，凸显充电基础设施的进步。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47427208' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47427208' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.qualys.com/vulnerabilities-threat-research/2026/03/17/cve-2026-3888-important-snap-flaw-enables-local-privilege-escalation-to-root' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CVE-2026-3888: Important Snap Flaw Enables Local Privilege Escalation to Root</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CVE-2026-3888：重要的 Snap 漏洞导致本地提权到 root</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 08:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47427208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47427208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47427208'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical vulnerability in Snap allows local privilege escalation to root, enabling attackers with limited access to gain full system control. The advisory urges updating to patched Snap versions and reviewing installed snaps for elevated permissions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Snap 存在关键漏洞，利用该缺陷可进行本地提权至 root，攻击者在有限权限下也能取得系统控制权限。建议更新到修补版本并检查已安装的 Snap 是否具备提升权限的配置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ik.imagekit.io/qualys/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Blog-Images-1080x1080.snap_.vuln_-1-scaled.png' data-full='https://ik.imagekit.io/qualys/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Blog-Images-1080x1080.snap_.vuln_-1-scaled.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47482825' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47482825' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nikomatsakis.github.io/rust-project-perspectives-on-ai/feb27-summary.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Diverse perspectives on AI from Rust contributors and maintainers</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47424592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47424592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47424592'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Parallel Perl introduces an autoparallelizing Perl interpreter with JIT, aiming to automatically exploit multicore CPUs without changing source code. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Parallel Perl 提供一个具备自动并行化能力的 Perl 解释器，结合 JIT 尝试在不修改代码的前提下自动利用多核。文章探讨了实现思路、潜在性能收益以及动态语言在依赖关系未显式表达时面临的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47456987'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A micro analysis shows about 90% of crypto-funded Illinois primary spending missed its objective, raising questions about funding effectiveness and influence in elections.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Revisits Lotus Notes, once a pioneering enterprise collaboration platform with built-in document databases and replication. Explores why it mattered for enterprise workflows and how its legacy influenced later collaboration tools and offline-first designs. Reflects on lessons for building durable, user-driven software ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾曾经的企业协作平台 Lotus Notes，它以内置文档数据库和数据复制等特性推动了工作流。分析它为何在企业中重要，以及其遗留对后续协作工具和离线优先设计的影响。从中提炼对构建稳健、用户驱动的软件生态的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47487584' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/02/10/github_outages/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub appears to be struggling with measly three nines availability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 的可用性似乎仅有 99.9%（三九），引发关注</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 23, 2026 / 03:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47487584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47487584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47487584'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub appears to be facing reliability issues despite being a core platform for developers, raising concerns about SLOs and uptime for massive code and CI pipelines. The outages stress the need for stronger resilience planning and incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 作为开发者的核心平台，正面临可靠性问题，令大规模代码托管与持续集成流水线的可用性成为焦点。事件暴露出在弹性设计和应急响应方面的不足，强调需要更强的容错能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://regmedia.co.uk/2022/02/15/outage_shutterstock.jpg' data-full='https://regmedia.co.uk/2022/02/15/outage_shutterstock.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47420950' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47420950' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.keithcirkel.co.uk/too-much-color/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Too Much Color</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>太多颜色</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 19:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47420950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47420950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47420950'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author warns that oversized color palettes in design systems create cognitive load, inconsistencies, and accessibility pitfalls, advocating for fewer semantic tokens and stronger contrast rules. Simplifying color usage helps teams move faster and produce more coherent, accessible interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者提醒，设计系统中过多的颜色会增加认知负担、导致风格不一致并带来可访问性问题，主张采用更少的语义化色彩令牌和更严格的对比规范。简化色彩使用能让团队更高效并产出更统一、易用的界面。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.keithcirkel.co.uk/images/preview/too-much-color.png' data-full='https://www.keithcirkel.co.uk/images/preview/too-much-color.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47385579' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47385579' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://exclusivearchitecture.com/03-technical-articles-CSDS-00-table-of-contents.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Six ingenious ways how Canon DSLRs used to illuminate their autofocus points</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>佳能数码单反在低光环境下照亮对焦点的六种巧妙用法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 02:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47385579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47385579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47385579'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canon DSLRs have used various tricks to illuminate autofocus points in low light, from built-in assist lamps to post-processing cues. The article outlines six clever techniques designers built into or around the cameras to help achieve sharp focus when ambient light is poor.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>佳能数码单反在低光环境下帮助对焦的照明方式有多种设计，从内置对焦辅助灯到光线外设的应用。文章梳理了六种巧妙的技术手段，帮助相机在昏暗场景中实现更锐利的对焦。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://exclusivearchitecture.com/images/main_logo_large.png' data-full='https://exclusivearchitecture.com/images/main_logo_large.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47421749' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ai.meta.com/research/publications/omnilingual-mt-machine-translation-for-1600-languages/?_fb_noscript=1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta&#x27;s Omnilingual MT for 1,600 Languages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 的 Omnilingual MT 支持1600种语言的机器翻译</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 22:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47421749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47421749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47421749'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta unveils Omnilingual MT, a multilingual machine translation model designed to handle 1,600 languages in a single system. By pooling data across languages, it aims to improve coverage for underrepresented tongues and reduce translation gaps, though performance may vary by language. The deployment could reshape access to information for many communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 公开 Omnilingual MT，一套面向1600种语言的通用翻译模型。通过跨语言数据整合提升对小语种的覆盖，尽管不同语言的翻译质量差异可能较大，但长期有望扩大信息获取的平等性。实际应用前景需关注数据规模与偏见等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scontent-atl3-3.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t39.8562-6/252294889_575082167077436_6034106545912333281_n.svg/meta-logo-primary_standardsize.svg?_nc_cat=108&amp;ccb=1-7&amp;_nc_sid=e280be&amp;_nc_ohc=7qFvHT2H62IQ7kNvwFlnpBX&amp;_nc_oc=AdphJSx9pPm0goLK3rg127z52NVQtXibfIU_daEg3-LTHGH64G9XFJx-UOZHSZg7QYo&amp;_nc_zt=14&amp;_nc_ht=scontent-atl3-3.xx&amp;_nc_gid=oC6zotDPb9yED3n5CSgVaQ&amp;_nc_ss=7a30f&amp;oh=00_AfyCAuWqz1FZMpQYG_lnlPQ_E6wn7pxOp0Gxn9LetJflZg&amp;oe=69C48279' data-full='https://scontent-atl3-3.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t39.8562-6/252294889_575082167077436_6034106545912333281_n.svg/meta-logo-primary_standardsize.svg?_nc_cat=108&amp;ccb=1-7&amp;_nc_sid=e280be&amp;_nc_ohc=7qFvHT2H62IQ7kNvwFlnpBX&amp;_nc_oc=AdphJSx9pPm0goLK3rg127z52NVQtXibfIU_daEg3-LTHGH64G9XFJx-UOZHSZg7QYo&amp;_nc_zt=14&amp;_nc_ht=scontent-atl3-3.xx&amp;_nc_gid=oC6zotDPb9yED3n5CSgVaQ&amp;_nc_ss=7a30f&amp;oh=00_AfyCAuWqz1FZMpQYG_lnlPQ_E6wn7pxOp0Gxn9LetJflZg&amp;oe=69C48279' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/google-deepmind/measuring-agi-cognitive-framework/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Measuring progress toward AGI: A cognitive framework</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>衡量通往通用人工智能（AGI）的进展：一个认知框架</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 04:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47424458' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47424458' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47424458'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google DeepMind proposes a cognitive framework to measure progress toward AGI, outlining metrics and benchmarks that assess cross-domain reasoning, learning efficiency, and robustness. The framework prompts ongoing conversations about how to evaluate intelligence in machines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌 DeepMind 提出一个用于衡量通往 AGI 进展的认知框架，概述评估跨领域推理、学习效率与鲁棒性的指标与基准。该框架推动对机器智能评估方法的持续讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47400261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47400261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apideck launches a CLI-based AI-agent interface that uses far smaller context windows than MCP. The approach reduces prompt sizing overhead and accelerates iterative AI-driven workflows for developers integrating APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apideck 推出基于命令行的 AI 代理接口，使用比 MCP 更小的上下文窗口。此举降低提示规模开销，加速在 API 集成中的 AI 驱动工作流。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(280) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/creationix/rx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RX – a new random-access JSON alternative</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RX：一种新的随机访问 JSON 替代方案</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 16:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47432915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47432915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47432915'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RX offers a data format with random-access capabilities that aims to outperform JSON on certain workloads, enabling direct access to nested structures without full parsing. It uses a binary layout with indexing and streaming semantics, suited for data intensive apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RX 提供具备随机访问能力的新的数据格式，旨在在某些工作负载上优于 JSON，实现对嵌套结构的直接访问而无需完整解析。它采用二进制格式并带有索引与流式语义，适用于数据密集型应用，但需配套的工具链和设计模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/52e2d477b160dfb015b3f1aa9d52196e1a6efd5ad7917fd39b3cc94b1df96dc8/creationix/rx' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/52e2d477b160dfb015b3f1aa9d52196e1a6efd5ad7917fd39b3cc94b1df96dc8/creationix/rx' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 11, 2026 / 16:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47343902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47343902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47343902'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A foundational look at floating-point arithmetic, outlining issues such as rounding, representation limits, and error propagation. It emphasizes numerical stability and provides practical guidance for writing robust, portable numerical code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概述浮点数运算中的常见陷阱，如舍入误差、表示范围和误差积累，强调数值稳定性的重要性，并给出编写健壮、可移植数值代码的实用建议。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用微积分解决数论问题</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47399330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47399330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47399330'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores using calculus-inspired methods, particularly Hensel&#x27;s Lemma and p-adic analysis, to tackle number-theory questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探索用微积分相关方法来解决数论问题，特别是使用汉塞尔引理等工具以及 p 进分析，展示了将连续数学思想应用到离散结构的跨界思路。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47444460' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47444460' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mattkeeter.com/blog/2022-08-11-udp/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From Oscilloscope to Wireshark: A UDP Story (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从示波器到 Wireshark：UDP 的故事（2022）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 12:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47444460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47444460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47444460'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective tracing UDP&#x27;s evolution from simple, raw datagrams to modern packet analysis with tools like Wireshark. It highlights the trade-offs between speed and reliability and the ongoing innovations in debugging network traffic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 UDP 如何从简单的数据报发展到如今由 Wireshark 等工具支撑的网络包分析。强调在速度与可靠性之间的权衡，以及网络调试方法的持续创新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mattkeeter.com/blog/2022-08-11-udp/probes.jpg' data-full='https://www.mattkeeter.com/blog/2022-08-11-udp/probes.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 03:10 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47423694'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ACM&#x27;s 2025 Turing Award honors transformative work in quantum information science, marking a rare bridge between physics and computer science. The recognition signals growing research momentum and potential real-world impact from quantum technologies. For engineers, it&#x27;s a milestone that could accelerate investment, talent, and innovation in quantum computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年图灵奖表彰在量子信息科学领域的突破性工作，体现物理学与计算机科学的跨学科融合日益成熟。此荣誉反映量子技术正获得更大的研究投入和现实潜力，将影响未来的计算基础设施。对技术领域从业者而言，这是推动量子计算相关投资、人才与创新节奏的信号。</p>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 05:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47386581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47386581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47386581'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Signet fuses satellite imagery with weather data to autonomously track wildfire risk and spread, delivering real-time monitoring and alerts. The project showcases how accessible data and lightweight analytics can augment firefighting decision-making.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Signet 将卫星影像与气象数据融合，实现对野火的自主监测和风险评估，提供实时跟踪与预警。该方案展示了可用数据和简化分析在提升消防决策中的作用。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://oseifert.ch/blog/building-pgit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Pgit – A Git-like CLI backed by PostgreSQL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Pgit — 一个以 PostgreSQL 为后端的类 Git 的命令行工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 23:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47409222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47409222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47409222'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pgit offers a Git-like CLI backed by PostgreSQL, enabling versioned file storage with SQL-level querying and multi-tenant capabilities. It trades off conventional Git performance for strong data integrity and database-backed collaboration workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pgit 提供一个以 PostgreSQL 为后端的类 Git 的命令行界面，允许用 SQL 查询对版本化数据进行管理，并支持多租户协作。它在数据完整性和团队协作方面具有潜在优势，但可能在性能与生态兼容性上带来取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自今日起证书颁发机构将对 DNSSEC 的部署进行核验</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 15:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47392510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47392510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 236</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47392510'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Starting today, certificate authorities will verify DNSSEC deployment and correctness, tightening security around DNS resolution. The move should improve trust in domain integrity and encourage operators to enable DNSSEC.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自今日起，证书颁发机构将对 DNSSEC 的部署与正确性进行核验，加强域名解析的安全性。此举有望提升域名完整性的信任度，并促使运营方启用 DNSSEC。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.grepular.com/img/og-default.png?v=BQk80DfWRSqEt8E2' data-full='https://www.grepular.com/img/og-default.png?v=BQk80DfWRSqEt8E2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 17:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47473231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47473231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47473231'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JavaScript Is Enough argues that JS remains a remarkably capable platform for most web tasks and beyond, thanks to its ecosystem, tooling, and ubiquity. The piece challenges language-purity anxieties and invites readers to lean into pragmatic, monolingual development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张 JavaScript 已足以应对大多数 Web 任务，得益于丰富的生态、工具链与广泛应用范围。文章挑战对语言纯粹性的执着，提倡以务实为先的开发思路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://emi-h.com/articles/a-case-against-currying.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A case against currying</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对柯里化的反对意见</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 06:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47477090'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that currying can harm readability and debugging in real-world code, especially as function chains grow. The piece advocates pragmatic function design and cautions against overusing point-free styles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出在实际代码中，柯里化可能降低可读性和调试性，尤其当函数链日益复杂时。提倡更务实的函数设计，警惕过度使用点-free 风格。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(315) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.cornell.edu/stories/2026/03/electron-microscopy-shows-mouse-bite-defects-semiconductors' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Electron microscopy shows ‘mouse bite’ defects in semiconductors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电子显微镜显示半导体的‘鼠咬痕’缺陷</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 13, 2026 / 16:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47371404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47371404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47371404'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Electron microscopy reveals &#x27;mouse bite&#x27; defects in semiconductors, a morphology that lowers yield and reliability. Understanding these defects helps guide fabrication process improvements and materials research for more resilient devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>电子显微镜揭示半导体制造中的‘mouse bite’缺陷，这类形貌会降低产率与可靠性。相关发现将推动工艺改进与材料研究，以提升器件稳定性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.cornell.edu/sites/default/files/styles/story_thumbnail_lg/public/2026-03/semiconductor_defects.jpg?itok=jOXsxFwh' data-full='https://news.cornell.edu/sites/default/files/styles/story_thumbnail_lg/public/2026-03/semiconductor_defects.jpg?itok=jOXsxFwh' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47451429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47451429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A payment provider terminated service for the Free Software Foundation Europe, disrupting donations and membership payments and prompting concern about dependency on centralized payment processors. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家支付服务商终止了对自由软件基金会欧洲分支的服务，导致捐款和会费支付中断，并引发对依赖集中支付通道的担忧。该事件凸显了当金融通道被切断而缺乏透明救济时，对民间组织的潜在风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47432779' data-tags='Hardware,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47432779' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lemire.me/blog/2026/03/18/how-many-branches-can-your-cpu-predict/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How many branches can your CPU predict?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的 CPU 能预测多少个分支？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 16:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47432779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47432779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47432779'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the capacity of modern branch predictors, illustrating how many distinct branches a predictor can track and how history length and aliasing affect accuracy. The takeaway is that software and compiler teams should understand predictor limits when optimizing performance-sensitive code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨现代分支预测器的容量，展示预测器能追踪的不同分支数量，以及历史长度与别名效应如何影响准确性。结论是，优化性能敏感代码时需理解预测器的极限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lemire.me/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Capture-decran-le-2026-03-18-a-17.52.22.png' data-full='https://lemire.me/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Capture-decran-le-2026-03-18-a-17.52.22.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47425918' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47425918' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/joaoh82/rustunnel' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A ngrok-style secure tunnel server written in Rust and Open Source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 实现的 ngrok 风格安全隧道服务器，开源</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 07:00 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47414291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47414291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47414291'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Antfly presents a Go-based distributed system for cross-modal search with memory graphs. It showcases techniques for indexing multimodal data, distributed query processing, and graph-backed memory to support persistent, context-rich searches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Antfly 提供一个用 Go 编写的分布式跨模态搜索系统，结合记忆图实现持久化上下文。展示了对多模态数据建立索引、分布式查询处理以及基于图的记忆的实现思路。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://octetta.github.io/k-synth/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: What if your synthesizer was powered by APL (or a dumb K clone)?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：如果你的合成器由 APL（或一个简化的 K 克隆）驱动？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 05:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47386983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47386983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47386983'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>K-Synth explores powering a synthesizer with APL (or a K-like language), turning language-oriented DSP into a compact, expressive tool. It highlights language-based approaches to music synthesis and the limits of self-contained interpreters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>K-Synth 探索用 APL（或类似 K 的语言）驱动合成器，将以语言为中心的数字信号处理变成简洁而富表达力的工具，展示了语言驱动的音频合成思路及自包含解释器的边界。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47467566' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47467566' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(338) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/mar/20/mediahuis-suspends-senior-journalist-over-ai-generated-quotes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Senior European journalist suspended over AI-generated quotes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>资深欧洲记者因 AI 生成的引语被停职</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 07:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47467566'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A senior journalist was suspended after publishing quotes generated by AI, prompting questions about attribution and verification in journalism. The incident underscores growing ethical and verification challenges as AI becomes a tool in reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位资深欧洲记者因刊发由 AI 生成的引语而被停职，事件引发关于报道署名与证据核实的伦理讨论。此事凸显了 AI 作为新闻工具带来的新挑战：如何确保来源可核查与真实。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/f53f8e674f5b3c1fccaf19b9b0a8e24f0987ae2f/101_0_1499_1199/master/1499.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=5adaa12d59579fdcda49ac7f11363bd9' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/f53f8e674f5b3c1fccaf19b9b0a8e24f0987ae2f/101_0_1499_1199/master/1499.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=5adaa12d59579fdcda49ac7f11363bd9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47461018'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discontinuation and reinitiation of dual-labeled GLP-1 receptor agonists may raise cardiovascular risks, underscoring the importance of adherence in therapy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>停用并重新开始双标 GLP-1 受体激动剂可能增加心血管风险，强调坚持用药的重要性及潜在临床影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 12:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47480900'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Teaching Claude to QA a mobile app demonstrates how chat-based models can contribute to test planning, scenario generation, and bug triage. It shows practical approaches to injecting AI into QA workflows without sacrificing rigor.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>让 Claude 执行移动应用 QA 的尝试，展示了对话模型在测试计划、场景生成和缺陷分拣等环节的潜在作用。演示了将 AI 融入 QA 流程时可用的具体方法，同时强调需保持严格的质控。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47460154' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://world.hey.com/jason/the-bespoke-software-revolution-i-m-not-buying-it-4bfad9ec' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The bespoke software revolution? I&#x27;m not buying it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>定制软件革命？我不买账</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 13:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47460154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47460154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47460154'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>He argues that broad claims of a bespoke software revolution are overblown, noting barriers like cost, complexity, and vendor lock-in. The piece challenges the hype around tailor-made software replacing off-the-shelf solutions and advocates practical, incremental customization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者质疑所谓的定制化软件革命，指出成本、复杂性与供应商锁定等壁垒。文中质疑量身定制的软件能否取代现成方案，并提倡务实的渐进式定制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://world.hey.com/jason/avatar-9f11ce77d31e68e834c564594ee0bc32a005ee8d' data-full='https://world.hey.com/jason/avatar-9f11ce77d31e68e834c564594ee0bc32a005ee8d' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 10:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47428868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47428868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47428868'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tmux-IDE is an open-source terminal IDE that prioritizes agent-driven workflows inside a tmux session. It blends a lightweight terminal workspace with an IDE-like experience for developers who live in the terminal. A glimpse into how agents can orchestrate coding tasks in a minimal, reproducible setup.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tmux-IDE 是一个以代理驱动工作流为核心的开源终端集成开发环境，运行在 tmux 会话中。它为习惯在终端工作的开发者提供接近 IDE 的体验。展示了通过代理在一个简洁、可复现的环境中编排编码任务的思路。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于 AI 提升生产力对人员配置的影响展开讨论，普遍观点倾向于通过更好的工具和技能提升来提升产出，而非大规模裁员；重点在于将 AI 效益转化为产品和业务的杠杆。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Voygr offers a maps API designed for agents and AI apps, aiming to streamline location reasoning, routing, and geocoding for autonomous assistants. The product emphasizes speed and structured data to support decision-making.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Voygr 提供专为代理与 AI 应用设计的地图 API，简化位置推理、路由与地理编码，支持自主助手的决策过程。强调高效与结构化数据，便于集成。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(349) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.msn.com/en-us/news/us/a-new-bigfoot-documentary-helps-explain-our-conspiracy-minded-era/ar-AA1Yv6px' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new Bigfoot documentary helps explain our conspiracy-minded era</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47359010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47359010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Space-grade software faces extreme reliability demands; kernel panics can be mission-critical in orbit. The piece surveys how engineers stress-test kernels, simulate faults, and design robust recovery when ground-based debugging is limited by latency and isolation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>太空中的软件需要极高的可靠性，轨道上的内核崩溃可能影响任务。文章回顾工程师如何在有限的调试机会、长延迟和环境隔离的情况下，通过压力测试、仿真和鲁棒设计来应对崩溃。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47439718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47439718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47439718'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Consensus Board Game simulates distributed agreement, helping players grasp how consensus protocols reach agreement despite failures and adversarial conditions. It yields intuition for leader election, quorums, and fault tolerance in distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款共识棋盘游戏通过桌面对局模拟分布式达成一致的过程，帮助玩家理解在容错和故障环境下如何达成共识。它能直观呈现领导者选举、法定人数与容错等概念。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://matklad.github.io/assets/2026-03-19-consensus-board-game/blank.svg' data-full='https://matklad.github.io/assets/2026-03-19-consensus-board-game/blank.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47393225' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47393225' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(362) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/troubleshoot/microsoft-365-apps/excel/wrongly-assumes-1900-is-leap-year' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Excel incorrectly assumes that the year 1900 is a leap year</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Excel 错把1900年当作闰年</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 16:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47393225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47393225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47393225'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Excel&#x27;s historical date logic treats 1900 as a leap year for compatibility, causing subtle date calculation errors in spreadsheets. The quirk underlines the importance of understanding software-era assumptions when processing dates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Excel 为了向后兼容把1900年误认成闰年，导致日期计算出现微妙错误。这一怪癖强调在处理日期时要理解软件时代的假设。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/media/open-graph-image.png' data-full='https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/media/open-graph-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://firthemouse.github.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: GDSL – 800 line kernel: Lisp subset in 500, C subset in 1300</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：GDSL——800 行内核：500 行 Lisp 子集，1300 行 C 子集</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 15, 2026 / 08:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47388479'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GDSL demonstrates a tiny kernel: about 800 lines of C-like kernel code plus a Lisp subset (500 lines) and a C subset (1300 lines), highlighting the possibility of minimal OS design and its educational value. A proof-of-concept for compact systems programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GDSL 展示了一个 800 行内核的极简实现，辅以 500 行 Lisp 子集和 1300 行 C 子集，强调最小化系统的可能性与教育价值。这个概念证明了紧凑型系统编程的可行性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(365) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://trigger.dev/blog/how-trql-works' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How we give every user SQL access to a shared ClickHouse cluster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们如何让每个用户都能访问共享的 ClickHouse 集群的 SQL</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 08:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47414356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47414356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47414356'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A blog details a scalable approach to give each user SQL access to a shared ClickHouse cluster, balancing isolation and efficiency with a lightweight access layer. The technique simplifies multi-tenant analytics while raising considerations for security and resource governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇博文介绍通过轻量化接入层，让每个用户都能在共享的 ClickHouse 集群上执行 SQL，兼顾数据隔离与性能。该方法有助于降低运维成本与实现集中化分析，但也提出安全与资源治理方面的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(366) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/a-czech-man-used-this-stone-in-his-barns-foundations-it-turned-out-to-be-rare-bronze-age-spearhead-mold-180988339/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Czech Man&#x27;s Stone in Barn&#x27;s Foundations Is Rare Bronze Age Spearhead Mold</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>捷克男子谷仓地基中的石头竟是罕见青铜时代矛头模具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 05:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397862' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397862' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47397862'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A farmer&#x27;s barn stone turned out to be a mold used to cast Bronze Age spearheads, revealing a clever, practical artifact-sourcing during the era. The find sheds light on Bronze Age metalworking and local exchange networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一块出现在捷克男子谷仓地基中的石头，被认定是铸造青铜时代矛头的模具，揭示了当时的实用工具与冶铸工艺。该发现为了解青铜时代的冶金作业和地方交流网络提供线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/AV4p_aZgdbw-VqhWs0mhkIT3D-o=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(486x279:487x280)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/64/31/6431a5a9-9bff-4259-a26c-e3d3e71e487a/spearhead.png' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/AV4p_aZgdbw-VqhWs0mhkIT3D-o=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(486x279:487x280)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/64/31/6431a5a9-9bff-4259-a26c-e3d3e71e487a/spearhead.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47428117' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47428117' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.inngest.com/blog/node-worker-threads' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Node.js worker threads are problematic, but they work great for us</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Node.js 的工作线程有问题，但对我们很有效</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 09:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47428117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47428117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47428117'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines trade-offs of Node.js worker_threads, acknowledging potential complexities and pitfalls, yet showing they deliver needed parallelism for the authors&#x27; use cases. Includes pragmatic patterns that keep them reliable in production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析 Node.js worker_threads 的利弊，承认潜在的复杂性与风险，但在特定用例中确实带来所需的并行性，分享让其在生产中稳定运行的实用模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.inngest.com/assets/blog/node-worker-threads/cover.png' data-full='https://www.inngest.com/assets/blog/node-worker-threads/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47350759' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47350759' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.asimov.press/p/viral-capsids' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Are Viral Capsids Icosahedral?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>病毒衣壳为何呈二十面体对称？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 12, 2026 / 07:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47350759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47350759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47350759'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Viral capsids commonly adopt icosahedral symmetry to maximize enclosed volume with minimal genetic information. The geometry contributes to robust assembly and stability, informing nanotech designs that mimic viral architecture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>病毒衣壳为何呈现二十面体对称？这种几何结构能在最少的遗传信息下最大化所容纳体积并增强稳定性。此类对称性对自组装研究和纳米技术设计具有启发意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!8iQI!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F98bf5c87-d3ed-41d4-8110-8c8c1fdfe759_2000x1260.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!8iQI!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F98bf5c87-d3ed-41d4-8110-8c8c1fdfe759_2000x1260.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

It emphasizes AI-assisted literature reviews, citation management, and team collaboration. Takeaway: a dedicated research hub can boost productivity for engineers and academics.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47358958' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.osti.gov/biblio/3013883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Efficient sparse computations using linear algebra aware compilers (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用线性代数感知编译器实现高效稀疏计算</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 12, 2026 / 16:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47358958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47358958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47358958'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study shows how linear algebra aware compilers can emit highly optimized code for sparse matrices, delivering meaningful speedups in ML and scientific computing. It demonstrates that exposing algebraic structure to compilers can unlock performance without changing algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示，若编译器能理解稀疏矩阵的结构，便能产出更高效的向量化代码，从而显著提升机器学习与科学计算的性能。核心在于让编译器暴露代数结构，而无需改动算法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.osti.gov/assets/img/src/DOE_SC_OSTI_2025_Horizonal-IndustrialGray.svg' data-full='https://www.osti.gov/assets/img/src/DOE_SC_OSTI_2025_Horizonal-IndustrialGray.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.harudagondi.space/blog/torturing-rustc-by-emulating-hkts/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Torturing Rustc by Emulating HKTs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过模拟 HKTs 来折磨 Rustc</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 14, 2026 / 00:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47374197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47374197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47374197'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into emulating higher-kinded types (HKTs) in Rust, exploring compiler behavior, trait tricks, and the limits of type-level engineering. The write-up showcases how researchers stress test rustc and what that implies for Rust&#x27;s type system.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入探讨在 Rust 中模拟高阶类型（HKTs），揭示编译器行为、trait 技巧及类型系统的边界。文章展示了研究者如何对 rustc 进行压力测试，以及这对 Rust 类型系统的潜在影响。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47473178' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47473178' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://writings.hongminhee.org/2026/03/craft-alienation-llm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why craft-lovers are losing their craft</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么热爱手工的人正在失去手艺</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 21, 2026 / 17:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47473178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47473178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47473178'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that AI and LLMs are accelerating the erosion of traditional craft, as automation commodifies processes and dilutes artisanal skills; the piece calls for preserving craft through intent and community.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出AI与大型语言模型正在加速手工艺的流失，因为自动化将工艺流程商品化、削弱工匠技艺；文章呼吁通过坚持创作意图和社区来保护手艺。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://amturing.acm.org' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turing Award Honors Bennett and Brassard for Quantum Information Science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>图灵奖表彰 Bennett 与 Brassard 在量子信息科学领域的贡献</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The ACM Turing Award honors Bennett and Brassard for foundational work in quantum information science, notably quantum cryptography and communication protocols like BB84. The recognition underscores how theoretical breakthroughs translate into practical cybersecurity and computing technology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项由 ACM 授予的图灵奖表彰 Bennett 和 Brassard 在量子信息科学领域的奠基性工作，尤其是量子密码学与 BB84 等通信协议。此荣誉凸显理论突破向实际网络安全与计算技术的转化。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>七小时爆炸事件仍难以解释，调查人员正在分析仪器数据与目击证词以重建发生过程。此事凸显事件重建的挑战以及对早期数据的局限性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece discusses a trend where spam messages use vibe-coding to align with cultural trends, using memes, emojis, and tone to bypass filters and entice engagement; includes examples of how marketers exploit vibe signals in emails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论垃圾邮件如何利用情绪编码来贴近潮流、使用表情符号和口吻来诱导点击，甚至规避简单的过滤。给出具体实例并分析对邮箱安全和品牌信任的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kita, a YC W26 startup, automates credit review in emerging markets using data-driven risk models and streamlined workflows. The platform aims to unlock lending to underserved populations by reducing manual review time and increasing consistency.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47462061' data-tags='Politics,Business,Finance,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47462061' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(414) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/03/20/nx-s1-5750812/how-the-iran-war-threatens-global-food-supply' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the Iran war threatens global food supply</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗战争如何威胁全球粮食供应</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 16:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47462061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47462061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47462061'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analyses explain how conflict involving Iran could disrupt grain trade, shipping routes, and energy markets, amplifying price volatility and food insecurity worldwide.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析指出，涉及伊朗的冲突可能扰乱粮食贸易、航运路线与能源市场，进而放大全球粮食价格波动与粮食安全风险。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces SpiceCrypt, a Python tool that decrypts LTspice-encrypted model files, enabling users to inspect and reuse proprietary models. The project lowers barriers for hardware researchers while raising questions about licensing and ethics.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47424881' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(421) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.shayon.dev/post/2026/65/linux-page-faults-mmap-and-userfaultfd/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux Page Faults, MMAP, and userfaultfd for faster VM boots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 页面错误、MMAP 与 userfaultfd 提速虚拟机启动</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 05:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47424881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47424881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47424881'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Techniques using Linux page faults, memory mapping, and the userfaultfd mechanism to accelerate virtual machine boots are explored. The approach reduces boot time by handling faults lazily and prefetching critical pages, addressing virtualization latency in cloud workloads. This matters for operators seeking faster scale-up and more responsive VMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨使用 Linux 页面错误、MMAP 和 userfaultfd 等机制加速虚拟机启动的方法。通过惰性处理页错误和对关键页的预取，降低启动延迟，降低云环境的虚拟化开销。对于需要快速弹性扩容和更高响应性的云工作负载尤为相关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.shayon.dev/page-fault.jpg' data-full='https://www.shayon.dev/page-fault.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47356782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47356782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47341995' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47341995' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dashbit.co/blog/type-systems-are-leaky-abstractions-map-take' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Type systems are leaky abstractions: the case of Map.take!/2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>类型系统是漏出的抽象：Map.take!/2 的案例</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 11, 2026 / 14:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47341995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47341995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47341995'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that type systems cannot capture all invariants, using Elixir&#x27;s Map.take!/2 as a case study to illustrate how practical coding can violate types and require runtime checks. It discusses design implications for language builders and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文认为类型系统无法覆盖所有不变性，以 Elixir 的 Map.take!/2 为案例，说明实际编码可能突破类型并需要运行时检查。讨论了对语言设计者与开发者的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of languages and styles that do not require semicolons, and what that design choice means for readability, tooling, and consistency. The takeaway is that syntax simplicity can trade off with discipline in code organization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评述不需要分号的语言与写法，以及该设计选择对可读性、工具链与代码一致性的影响。结论是语法简化有利于易读性，但也需要良好的代码组织约束。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 08:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47427290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47427290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>民众认定 AI 是财富分配不平等的放大器，民调有此发现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 10:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47428335' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47428335' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Polls show Americans view AI as exacerbating wealth inequality, with concerns that automation and AI-enabled productivity will disproportionately benefit high-skilled workers and capital owners. The piece outlines public sentiment and potential policy implications for education, labor, and taxes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>民调显示美国人普遍认为 AI 会加剧财富不平等，担心自动化和 AI 生产力提升将主要惠及高技能工人和资本所有者。文章探讨了这一公众观点及对教育、劳动力市场与税制的潜在政策含义。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47407184'>
