<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47484519' data-tags='Culture,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47484519' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(435) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.joanwestenberg.com/collaboration-is-bullshit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;Collaboration&quot; Is Bullshit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>合作就是废话</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 18:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47484519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47484519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47484519'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues that collaboration is often overhyped and inefficient; suggests alternatives: focusing on clear ownership, autonomous teams, and better decision protocols.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者批评过度强调协作，认为实际工作中协作常常拖慢进度，提出以独立承担、明确的所有权和高效的协调机制来替代无休止的协作。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47431304' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(441) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mydbanotebook.org/posts/work_mem-its-a-trap/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Work_mem: It&#x27;s a Trap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Work_mem：这是个陷阱</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 18, 2026 / 13:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47431304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47431304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47431304'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post warns that increasing work_mem in PostgreSQL can backfire by consuming too much RAM during parallel queries, leading to swaps or OOM. The piece advocates careful benchmarking, realistic workloads, and avoiding blanket defaults.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章警告 PostgreSQL 的 work_mem 设置过高可能在并发查询中吞掉大量内存，导致换出或内存不足。建议进行小规模基准测试、使用实际工作负载，并避免一刀切的默认值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47396340' data-tags='Data,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47396340' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(446) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/retro-gaming/385tb-video-game-archive-saved-by-fans-myrient-has-been-100-percent-backed-up-and-validated-torrents-being-generated' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>385TB video game archive saved by fans; torrents being generated</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>爱好者拯救了385TB的视频游戏档案，正在生成种子</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 01:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47396340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47396340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47396340'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fans have backed up a 385 TB archive of games, with torrents being seeded to ensure redundancy and verifiability. The effort highlights community-driven digital preservation and the practical challenges of long-term archival at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>爱好者完成了一个385TB的视频游戏档案的备份工作，正在生成种子以实现冗余和验证。此举凸显社区驱动的数字保存挑战与解决思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/5hGmDiVdo9DpPaeKnQ7eSb-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/5hGmDiVdo9DpPaeKnQ7eSb-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47420619' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(447) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/robotocore/robotocore' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Robotocore · a Digital Twin of AWS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Robotocore：AWS 的数字孪生</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 18:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47420619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47420619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47420619'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Robotocore provides a digital twin of AWS, enabling offline testing and simulation of cloud workloads. By modeling cloud infrastructure as code, it helps operators prototype changes with reduced risk and cost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Robotocore 提供了 AWS 的数字孪生，便于在本地离线仿真云工作负载、测试基础设施变更，降低成本与风险。该开源项目推动云原生环境的本地化开发与试验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d04b4ae8027d0ba4c0f8d4e54e4a370fe96707f0b953ac7be1730a153a4ce7d5/robotocore/robotocore' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d04b4ae8027d0ba4c0f8d4e54e4a370fe96707f0b953ac7be1730a153a4ce7d5/robotocore/robotocore' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(450) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://japannews.yomiuri.co.jp/society/general-news/20260319-317449/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Heisuke Hironaka Has Died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>著名数学家弘中英機去世</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 01:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47436287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47436287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47436287'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Heisuke Hironaka, renowned for work on resolution of singularities and a Fields Medalist, has died, marking a significant loss in algebraic geometry. His contributions shaped modern theory and computational methods.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>著名数学家弘中英機因在代数几何中的奇点消解理论作出奠基性贡献而知名，曾获菲尔兹奖。其工作深刻影响了现代代数几何及相关计算方法。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(451) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.embarcadero.com/announcing-the-availability-of-rad-studio-13-florence-update-1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Delphi 13.1 Released, with ARM64 support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Delphi 13.1 发布，新增 ARM64 支持</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 04:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47453261'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The new Delphi 13.1 release adds ARM64 support, enabling native compilation for modern ARM platforms and expanding Delphi&#x27;s cross‑platform reach. This update is aimed at developers targeting performance‑sensitive or mobile/embedded ARM targets while keeping the existing RAD tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Delphi 13.1 新版加入了 ARM64 支持，允许为现代 ARM 平台生成原生可执行文件，扩展了 Delphi 的跨平台能力。该更新面向需要高性能或面向移动/嵌入式 ARM 目标的开发者，同时保留现有的快速应用开发工具链。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines the historic tension between ASCII quotes and typographic Unicode quotes, focusing on encoding, rendering, and cross-system compatibility. It offers practical guidance for choosing safe quotes in code, data interchange, and publishing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 ASCII 引号与 Unicode 引号的历史性问题，讨论在文本编码、渲染和跨系统互操作时的兼容性陷阱。给出在代码、数据交换与发布中选用引号的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(453) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tldr/897566/marc-andreessen-is-a-philosophical-zombie' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Marc Andreessen is a philosophical zombie</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马克·安德森是哲学僵尸</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 19, 2026 / 13:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47445593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47445593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47445593'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece contends that Marc Andreessen embodies a philosophical persona detached from actionable results, critiquing the tech industry’s focus on grand ideas over execution. It invites reflection on how such figures shape policy and investment culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为马克·安德森展现出一种与实际行动脱节的哲学化形象，批评科技行业过于追逐宏大理念而缺乏执行力。提醒读者思考此类人物如何影响政策与投资文化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/03/268412_Marc_Andreessen_is_a_philosophical_zombie_CVirginia2.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/03/268412_Marc_Andreessen_is_a_philosophical_zombie_CVirginia2.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 14, 2026 / 01:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47374519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47374519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project by the British Library to restore the earliest known computer-generated music recording, preserving a historic intersection of computer science and music. The restoration sheds light on early digital audio synthesis and the challenges of preserving old media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国图书馆的一个项目修复了已知最早的计算机音乐录音，展示计算机科学与音乐交叉的历史。修复工作揭示了早期数字音频合成的历史细节与保存老旧媒介的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中回顾美国运通支付网络的两次迁移，分析在迁移高可用金融系统时的挑战、架构取舍与对开发者和商家的影响。强调在支付网络迁移中需关注 API 设计、数据迁移和风控等关键要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47452248' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47452248' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(467) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/mar/19/essex-police-pause-facial-recognition-camera-use-study-racial-bias' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Essex police pause facial recognition camera use after study finds racial bias</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>埃塞克斯警方在研究发现种族偏差后暂停面部识别摄像头使用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 20, 2026 / 02:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47452248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47452248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47452248'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Police halted live facial recognition camera deployments after a study found racial bias and high error rates, reflecting growing scrutiny of biometric surveillance. The pause follows concerns that such systems misidentify minorities and lack sufficient governance to prevent harms in policing contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在一项研究发现面部识别存在种族偏差和较高错误率后，警方暂停了实时面部识别摄像头的使用，显示对生物识别监控的审查日益加强。暂停措施源于该类系统易误识少数群体且治理不足，可能在执法场景中造成伤害。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/9f9926cae92981cf378aea4c4a5da4278456c540/308_0_6882_5504/master/6882.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=ba94050367898cf0d98f78e830851e93' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/9f9926cae92981cf378aea4c4a5da4278456c540/308_0_6882_5504/master/6882.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=ba94050367898cf0d98f78e830851e93' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47434311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47434311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47434311'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Startup pundits keep regurgitating the same conventional wisdom even as markets and startups fail to match the old playbooks. It suggests founders should ignore hype, focus on unit economics, product-market fit, and real-world experimentation. The article urges a more disciplined, evidence-based approach to building ventures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章批评创业圈评论家仍复刻旧有教条，市场环境已变化却缺乏新见解。创始人应忽略噱头，专注单位经济、产品与市场匹配，以及现实世界的验证。呼吁以更有纪律、基于证据的方式来打造企业。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bernsteinbear.com/blog/toy-fuzzer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Fuzzer for the Toy Optimizer</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 17, 2026 / 08:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47414112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47414112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47414112'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A fuzzing approach targets a tiny optimizer to surface edge cases and stability issues, illustrating how automated input generation can improve even small-scale optimization code. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为一个小型优化器设计了模糊测试工具，用以暴露边界情况与稳定性问题，展示自动化输入生成在小型优化代码中的效用。该方法有助于及早发现缺陷与性能瓶颈，是可靠性工程的实用案例。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/embesozzi/keycloak-openfga-event-publisher' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Event Publisher enables event integration between Keycloak and OpenFGA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Event Publisher 让 Keycloak 与 OpenFGA 之间实现事件驱动的集成</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 07:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47399437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47399437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Event Publisher enables event-driven integration between Keycloak and OpenFGA, syncing identity and authorization decisions in real time. This helps enforce policy-driven access across apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Event Publisher 让 Keycloak 与 OpenFGA 之间实现事件驱动的集成，实时同步身份与授权决策，帮助跨应用执行策略驱动的访问控制。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47473884' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47473884' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ArXiv paper explores how vectorizing Verilog designs affects verification and synthesis, showing how bit-vector representations can impact equivalence checking and hardware synthesis results. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文探讨 Verilog 设计向量化对验证和综合的影响，提示位向量表示可能改变等价性检查与硬件综合结果。对硬件设计者而言，这些发现有助于提升验证性能和预测综合行为。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canopy Height Maps v2 upgrades canopy height data with improved algorithms and higher-resolution imagery, enabling better forest biomass estimation and carbon accounting. The update benefits climate science, forestry analytics, and conservation planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Canopy Height Maps v2 使用改进的算法和更高分辨率的数据，提升树冠高度信息的准确性，便于森林生物量估算和碳核算。对林业、气候与保护分析具有实际应用价值。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47469945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47469945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-title'>(488) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Linux-7.1-Retiring-UDP-Lite' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux 7.1 to Retire UDP-Lite – Allows for Better Performance with Cleansed Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 7.1 将停止支持 UDP-Lite，以简化代码和提升性能</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 16, 2026 / 03:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47397291'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The kernel maintainers plan to remove UDP-Lite in favor of simpler, faster UDP implementations, reducing edge-case handling and complexity. This move improves performance and security surface, but may affect users relying on UDP-Lite features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 7.1 将弃用 UDP-Lite，提升代码简化、性能与安全性。此举虽提升核心网络实现的效率，但可能影响依赖 UDP-Lite 的使用场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=retire_udp_lite' data-full='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=retire_udp_lite' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加拿大向本土火箭发射迈进</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canada is moving toward developing homegrown rocket launch capability, signaling investment in national space infrastructure. The move could diversify the space sector, attract startups, and reduce reliance on foreign launch providers, with implications for national security and sovereignty. Policy and funding details will shape timeline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加拿大正在推动本土火箭发射能力的发展，显示出对国家空间基础设施投资的决心。此举可能促进本地太空产业多元化、吸引初创公司并降低对外部发射服务的依赖，同时对国家安全与主权有一定影响。具体的政策与资金安排将决定时间表。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47480054' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(520) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thecanadianpressnews.ca/business/battle-of-the-rail-barons-how-a-merger-is-setting-the-industry-on-a-collision/article_4b82ef47-bb9b-533a-ac97-78a89d065ad1.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Battle of U.S. rail barons: Merger is setting the industry on a collision course</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美铁路巨头之战：合并让行业走向对峙</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Mar 22, 2026 / 10:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 5</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47480054'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The merger narrative among U.S. rail barons points to a collision course for the industry as consolidation reshapes pricing, capacity, and investment. Regulators and competitors watch closely as rail infrastructure and logistics trends tilt toward fewer, bigger players.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美铁路巨头合并潮让行业走向更高强度的竞争与重组，价格、运力和投资走向的变化备受关注。监管机构与竞争对手将密切关注这一整合带来的市场格局。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece introduces concept of Answer Engine Optimization focusing on structuring content for direct answers in search and AI chat, using schema, concise formatting, FAQs; advice for creators to tailor content to become authoritative answer sources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出答案引擎优化的概念，强调为搜索和对话式 AI 提供直接答案的内容结构化、FAQ 与模式化回答的优化策略，帮助创作者成为权威答案源。</p>
